# List 13

## Unit 1

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>impugn</b></td><td>/ɪmˈpjuːn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抨击，质疑（诚信、动机）</td><td>impugn someone's motives<br>质疑某人的动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He never impugned the honesty of his opponents.  他从不质疑对手的诚实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 attack</td><td>/əˈtæk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>攻击，抨击</td><td>attack his motives<br>攻击他的动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rival scholars attacked the credibility of her new evidence.  对立的学者抨击她新证据的可信度。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assail</td><td>/əˈseɪl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抨击，严厉批评</td><td>assail a policy<br>抨击某项政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Opponents assailed the plan as reckless and unfair.  反对者抨击该计划轻率且不公。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 contradict</td><td>/ˌkɑːntrəˈdɪkt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认，反驳；与…相矛盾</td><td>contradict the evidence<br>与证据相矛盾</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness's second statement flatly contradicted everything she had said earlier.  证人的第二份陈述与她此前所说完全矛盾。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 contravene</td><td>/ˌkɑːntrəˈviːn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>违反，反对</td><td>contravene the rules<br>违反规定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Building a factory here would contravene several local environmental regulations.  在此建厂会违反数条当地环保法规。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cross</td><td>/ˈkrɔːs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>反驳，阻挠；穿越</td><td>cross his plans<br>阻挠他的计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nobody dared cross the old chairman in public.  没人敢在公开场合反驳这位老董事长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disaffirm</del></td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈfɜrm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认，否定；废除</td><td>disaffirm the contract<br>否认合同效力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court may disaffirm a contract signed by a minor.  法院可以否定未成年人签署的合同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deny</td><td>/dɪˈnaɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认；拒绝给予</td><td>flatly deny the charge<br>断然否认指控</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I do not deny that the plan carries some very real risks.  我不否认这个计划确实带有一些实实在在的风险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gainsay</td><td>/ˌɡeɪnˈseɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认</td><td>gainsay the evidence<br>否认证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No one could gainsay the evidence the inspector had collected.  没有人能否认检查员收集到的证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 negate</td><td>/nɪˈɡeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认；否定，取消</td><td>negate the effect<br>抵消效果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One careless error can negate weeks of careful work.  一个粗心的错误能抹掉数周的细致工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 negative</td><td>/ˈneɡətɪv/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>否决，反驳；负面的</td><td>negative the proposal<br>否决这项提案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee negatived the motion after a short debate.  委员会经短暂辩论后否决了该动议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 traverse</td><td>/trəˈvɜːrs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>反驳，否认；横穿</td><td>traverse the allegation<br>反驳该指控</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The defense traversed every allegation made by the prosecution.  辩方否认了检方提出的每一项指控。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 advocate</td><td>/ˈædvəkeɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>支持，提倡</td><td>advocate reform<br>提倡改革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Many doctors advocate walking as the simplest form of exercise.  许多医生提倡把走路作为最简单的运动方式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 back</td><td>/ˈbæk/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>支持，资助</td><td>back a candidate<br>支持某位候选人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several investors agreed to back her new company.  几位投资人同意资助她的新公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 support</td><td>/səˈpɔːrt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>支持，支撑</td><td>support a proposal<br>支持一项提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most members voted to support the new proposal.  大多数成员投票支持这项新提案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 uphold</td><td>/ʌpˈhoʊld/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>支持，赞成；维持（原判）</td><td>uphold a decision<br>维持原判决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The appeals court chose to uphold the original ruling in full.  上诉法院决定完全维持原判。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>impuissance</b></del></td><td>/ɪmˈpjuːɪsəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>无权，虚弱</td><td>political impuissance<br>政治上的无权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee's impuissance became clear when nobody obeyed its order.  没人服从命令时，委员会的无权就显露了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impotence</td><td>/ˈɪmpətəns/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>无力，无能</td><td>a sense of impotence<br>无能为力之感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Voters expressed impotence in the face of rising prices.  面对物价上涨，选民表达了无能为力之感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 powerlessness</td><td>/ˈpaʊərləsnəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>无力，无权</td><td>a sense of powerlessness<br>无力感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Long unemployment bred a deep feeling of powerlessness.  长期失业催生了深深的无力感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 weakness</td><td>/ˈwiːknəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>弱点，缺点</td><td>a weakness for sweets<br>对甜食的偏好</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her main weakness is a reluctance to delegate work.  她的主要弱点是不愿把工作交给别人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 clout</td><td>/klaʊt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>权力，影响力</td><td>political clout<br>政治影响力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The union still has enough political clout to block the bill.  工会仍有足够的政治影响力阻止这项法案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 potency</td><td>/ˈpoʊtənsi/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>力量，效力</td><td>the potency of the drug<br>药物的效力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The vaccine loses potency if it is not kept cold.  疫苗若不冷藏就会失去效力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 power</td><td>/ˈpaʊər/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>权力，力量</td><td>come to power<br>掌权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party came to power after a narrow election victory.  该党以微弱优势胜选后上台执政。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 puissance</td><td>/ˈpwɪsəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>权力</td><td>the puissance of kings<br>君王的权势</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old chronicles celebrate the puissance of kings and their armies.  古老的编年史颂扬君王及其军队的权势。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>inadvertent</b></td><td>/ˌɪnədˈvɜːrtənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非故意的，疏忽的</td><td>an inadvertent error<br>无意间的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An inadvertent keystroke deleted an entire afternoon of careful work.  一次无意的按键删掉了整个下午的细致工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 casual</td><td>/ˈkæʒəwəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>随意的，非故意的</td><td>a casual remark<br>随口的一句话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A casual remark at dinner accidentally revealed the whole secret.  晚餐时随口一句话意外揭出了整个秘密。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fluky</td><td>/ˈfluːki/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>侥幸的</td><td>a fluky victory<br>侥幸的胜利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A fluky coincidence put me indoors when the blizzard finally struck.  一个侥幸的巧合让暴风雪来袭时我正待在室内。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incidental</td><td>/ˌɪnsɪˈdentəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>偶然的；附带的</td><td>incidental expenses<br>杂项开支</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The damage was incidental to the main purpose of the test.  这种损坏只是测试主要目的之外的附带结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unintentional</td><td>/ˌʌnɪnˈtenʃənəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无意的，非故意的</td><td>an unintentional insult<br>无意的冒犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Any offence caused by my remark was entirely unintentional.  我的话若造成任何冒犯，完全是无意的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unplanned</td><td>/ənˈplænd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无计划的，意外的</td><td>an unplanned detour<br>计划外的绕行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An unplanned stop in the village became the trip's highlight.  在村里的一次临时停留成了旅行的亮点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unpremeditated</td><td>/ˌʌnpriˈmedɪteɪtɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非预谋的，未事先计划的</td><td>an unpremeditated attack<br>非预谋的袭击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court decided the killing was sudden and unpremeditated.  法庭认定这起杀人是突发且非预谋的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unwitting</td><td>/ʌnˈwɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不知道的，未觉察的</td><td>an unwitting accomplice<br>不知情的帮凶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She became an unwitting participant in the whole scheme.  她不知不觉地成了整个阴谋的参与者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 careless</td><td>/ˈkerləs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗心的，疏忽的</td><td>a careless mistake<br>粗心的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One careless remark ruined months of patient negotiation.  一句不慎的话毁了数月的耐心谈判。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 feckless</td><td>/ˈfekləs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无成果的，没有价值的；粗心不负责任的</td><td>a feckless young man<br>不成事的年轻人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His feckless handling of the accounts nearly ruined the shop.  他对账目粗心不负责的处理几乎毁了这家店。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 heedless</td><td>/ˈhiːdləs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不注意的，不顾的</td><td>heedless of danger<br>不顾危险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He ran into the street heedless of the traffic.  他不顾车流冲进了街道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 irreflective</del></td><td>/ˌɪrɪˈflektɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不加思索的，欠考虑的</td><td>an irreflective habit<br>不经思考的习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His irreflective answer showed he had not read the report.  他不加思索的回答说明他没读过报告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 thoughtless</td><td>/ˈθɔːtləs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>欠考虑的，轻率的</td><td>a thoughtless remark<br>轻率的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was thoughtless of him to slam the door at midnight.  他半夜摔门实在是欠考虑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uncaring</td><td>/ənˈkerɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不关心的，冷漠的</td><td>an uncaring attitude<br>漠不关心的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She described the hospital staff as cold and uncaring.  她形容医院工作人员冷漠而不关心人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 calculated</td><td>/ˈkælkjuleɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>经过深思熟虑的，有意的</td><td>a calculated risk<br>经过权衡的风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Investing now was a calculated risk that finally paid off.  现在投资是经过盘算的风险，最终有了回报。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deliberate</td><td>/dɪˈlɪbərət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>深思熟虑的，故意的</td><td>a deliberate attempt<br>故意的尝试</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The delay was deliberate, designed to weaken our position.  这次延误是故意的，目的是削弱我们的立场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 intended</td><td>/ɪnˈtendɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>预期的，有意的</td><td>the intended audience<br>目标读者群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The book never reached its intended audience of young readers.  这本书从未触及它预期的年轻读者群。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 intentional</td><td>/ɪnˈtenʃənəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>故意的，有意的</td><td>an intentional foul<br>故意犯规</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The omission was intentional rather than a simple oversight.  这一遗漏是故意的，而非单纯的疏忽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 planned</td><td>/ˈplænd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>计划好的，有计划的</td><td>a carefully planned trip<br>精心策划的旅行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole ceremony was planned down to the last detail.  整个典礼被计划到了最后一个细节。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 premeditated</td><td>/priˈmedəˌteɪtɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>预谋的，事先策划的</td><td>premeditated murder<br>预谋杀人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Prosecutors argued that the robbery was carefully premeditated.  检察官认为这起抢劫是精心预谋的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 advertent</del></td><td>/ədˈvɜːrtənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>注意的，留意的</td><td>advertent to risks<br>留意风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A good editor stays advertent to the smallest inconsistency.  好编辑对最细微的不一致也保持留意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 careful</td><td>/ˈkerfəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>谨慎的，仔细的</td><td>careful consideration<br>慎重考虑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Be careful when you cross this busy street at night.  夜里穿过这条繁忙的街道时要小心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 heedful</del></td><td>/ˈhiːdfəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>留心的，注意的</td><td>heedful of the warning<br>留意警告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heedful of the forecast, the climbers turned back early.  留意到天气预报，登山者提早返回了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mindful</td><td>/ˈmaɪndfəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>留心的，注意的</td><td>mindful of the risks<br>留意风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Be mindful of the time when you answer each question.  回答每个问题时要留意时间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>inalienable</b></td><td>/ɪnˈeɪliənəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可剥夺的，不能让与的</td><td>inalienable rights<br>不可剥夺的权利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The constitution describes these freedoms as inalienable rights of every citizen.  宪法称这些自由是每个公民不可剥夺的权利。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 untransferable</del></td><td>/ˌʌntrænsˈfɜːrəbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可转让的</td><td>an untransferable ticket<br>不可转让的票</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This scholarship is personal and completely untransferable.  这项奖学金属于个人，完全不可转让。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 alienable</td><td>/ˈeɪliənəbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可让与的，可转让的</td><td>alienable property rights<br>可转让的财产权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Under the new law, such land became freely alienable.  依照新法，这类土地变得可以自由转让。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>inane</b></td><td>/ɪˈneɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>空洞的</td><td>inane chatter<br>空洞的闲聊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film's dialogue was inane and endlessly repetitive.  这部电影的对白空洞且不断重复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 empty</td><td>/ˈempti/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>空洞的；空的</td><td>empty promises<br>空洞的承诺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His speech was full of empty phrases about progress.  他的演讲满是关于进步的空洞辞藻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insubstantial</td><td>/ˌɪnsəbˈstænʃəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非实在的；虚弱的</td><td>insubstantial evidence<br>站不住脚的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His case rested on insubstantial rumor rather than real documents.  他的论据靠的是虚无的传言而非真实文件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pointless</td><td>/ˈpɔɪntləs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无意义的，徒劳的</td><td>a pointless argument<br>毫无意义的争论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Arguing with him about politics is completely pointless and exhausting.  和他争论政治完全没有意义，而且很累。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 senseless</td><td>/ˈsensləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无意义的，愚蠢的</td><td>senseless violence<br>毫无意义的暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cutting the safety budget now would be completely senseless.  现在削减安全预算完全是愚蠢之举。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 meaningful</td><td>/ˈmiːnɪŋfəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有意义的，意味深长的</td><td>meaningful work<br>有意义的工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She left banking to find more meaningful work.  她离开银行业去寻找更有意义的工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 significant</td><td>/səɡˈnɪfɪkənt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>重要的；显著的</td><td>a significant difference<br>显著的差别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers found a significant drop in the pollution levels.  研究者发现污染水平显著下降。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>inanimate</b></td><td>/ɪnˈænɪmət/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无生命的</td><td>an inanimate object<br>无生命的物体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Children often speak to inanimate objects as though they could reply.  孩子常对无生命的物体说话，仿佛它们能够回答。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dead</td><td>/ˈded/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>死的；毫无生气的</td><td>a dead town at night<br>夜里死寂的小镇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The resort is completely dead in the winter months.  这个度假地在冬季几个月里毫无生气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lifeless</td><td>/ˈlaɪfləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无生命的；无生气的</td><td>a lifeless performance<br>毫无生气的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The actors gave a lifeless performance to a bored audience.  演员们在无聊的观众前演得毫无生气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insensible</td><td>/ɪnˈsensəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无知觉的，昏迷的</td><td>insensible to pain<br>感觉不到疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The blow left him insensible for several minutes.  这一击使他几分钟内失去了知觉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 insentient</del></td><td>/ɪnˈsentiənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无感觉的，无知觉的</td><td>insentient matter<br>无知觉的物质</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ancient philosophers debated whether plants are insentient or capable of feeling pain.  古代哲学家争论植物是无知觉的还是能感到痛苦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 senseless</td><td>/ˈsensləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无意义的，愚蠢的</td><td>senseless violence<br>毫无意义的暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cutting the safety budget now would be completely senseless.  现在削减安全预算完全是愚蠢之举。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unfeeling</td><td>/ənˈfiːlɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无感情的，冷酷的</td><td>an unfeeling response<br>冷漠的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It seemed unfeeling to laugh at such a moment.  在这样的时刻发笑显得毫无感情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 animate</td><td>/ˈænəmeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>使有生气，激励；有活力的</td><td>animate the discussion<br>让讨论活跃起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A few well-chosen questions can animate an otherwise dull discussion.  几个精心挑选的问题就能让本来沉闷的讨论活跃起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 living</td><td>/ˈlɪvɪŋ/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>活着的，在世的</td><td>the greatest living writer<br>在世最伟大的作家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Coelacanths were long thought extinct until a living one was caught.  矛尾鱼长期被认为已灭绝，直到捕到一条活的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>inaugurate</b></td><td>/ɪˈnɔːɡjəreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>开始</td><td>inaugurate a new era<br>开创一个新时代</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treaty inaugurated a long period of peace between the two nations.  这项条约开启了两国之间长期的和平。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 begin</td><td>/bɪˈɡɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>开始，着手</td><td>begin work on the project<br>开始着手这个项目</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We will begin the renovation as soon as the permit arrives.  许可一到我们就开始装修。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 commence</td><td>/kəˈmens/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>开始，着手（正式）</td><td>commence legal proceedings<br>开始法律诉讼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Construction will commence next month if funding is approved.  若资金获批，工程将于下月开始。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 establish</td><td>/ɪˈstæblɪʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>确证，证实；建立</td><td>establish the facts beyond doubt<br>确证事实无疑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The investigation established the facts beyond any reasonable doubt.  调查毫无疑问地确证了事实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 institute</td><td>/ˈɪnstɪtuːt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>创立，制定；机构，学院</td><td>institute legal proceedings<br>提起法律诉讼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school instituted a new dress code this term.  学校本学期制定了新的着装规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 introduce</td><td>/ˌɪntrəˈduːs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>引入，开始实行；介绍</td><td>introduce a new policy<br>推行一项新政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city introduced free bus travel for elderly residents.  该市开始为老年居民提供免费公交。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 launch</td><td>/ˈlɔːntʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>发起，开始；发射</td><td>launch a new campaign<br>发起新的活动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company plans to launch its cheapest model this autumn.  公司计划今秋推出其最便宜的型号。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 open</td><td>/ˈoʊpən/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>公开的，坦率的</td><td>open opposition<br>公开的反对</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was open opposition to the plan at the meeting.  会上有人公开反对这项计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 plant</td><td>/ˈplænt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>种植，栽种；植物；工厂</td><td>plant a row of trees<br>栽种一排树</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Volunteers came to plant young oaks along the muddy riverbank.  志愿者来到泥泞的河岸栽种小橡树。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 start</td><td>/ˈstɑːrt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>开始，开端</td><td>from the start<br>从一开始</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 From the start it was clear the schedule was too tight.  从一开始就明显看出日程太紧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cease</td><td>/ˈsiːs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>停止，终止</td><td>cease all operations<br>停止一切运作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company will cease production at that plant in June.  公司将于六月停止该厂的生产。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 close</td><td>/kloʊz/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>关闭；结束</td><td>close the meeting<br>结束会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They closed the discussion before anyone could object.  他们在有人反对之前结束了讨论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 end</td><td>/ˈend/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>结束；末端</td><td>end the war<br>结束战争</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The strike ended after both sides agreed on wages.  双方就工资达成一致后罢工结束。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 terminate</td><td>/ˈtɜːrməˌneɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>终止，结束</td><td>terminate the agreement<br>终止协议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The club terminated his membership without any explanation.  俱乐部未加解释就终止了他的会员资格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>incandescent</b></td><td>/ˌɪnkænˈdesənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>白热的，耀眼的</td><td>an incandescent bulb<br>白炽灯泡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The incandescent filament burned out after only a few weeks.  白炽灯丝仅用几周就烧断了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 beaming</td><td>/ˈbiːmɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>光芒四射的；笑容满面的</td><td>a beaming smile<br>灿烂的笑容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She greeted us with a beaming smile at the airport gate.  她在机场门口用灿烂的笑容迎接我们。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brilliant</td><td>/ˈbrɪljənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>聪颖的；辉煌的</td><td>a brilliant student<br>才华出众的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was a brilliant mathematician long before she turned twenty.  她还没到二十岁就已是出色的数学家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dazzling</td><td>/ˈdæzlɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>炫目的，耀眼的</td><td>a dazzling display<br>炫目的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dancers wore dazzling costumes that glittered under the stage lights.  舞者们穿着在舞台灯光下闪耀的炫目服装。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 effulgent</td><td>/ɪˈfʌldʒənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>光辉灿烂的</td><td>an effulgent sunrise<br>灿烂的日出</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An effulgent sunrise turned the snow on the eastern ridges pale gold.  灿烂的日出把东侧山脊上的积雪染成浅金色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 glowing</td><td>/ˈɡloʊɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>发光的，炽热的</td><td>glowing embers<br>发光的余烬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Glowing coals gave the little room a warm orange light.  发光的炭火给小屋带来温暖的橙色光线。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lucent</td><td>/ˈluːsɪnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>明亮的，透明的</td><td>lucent morning light<br>明亮的晨光</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lake lay lucent under a cloudless autumn sky.  湖水在无云的秋空下清亮透明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 luminous</td><td>/ˈluːmɪnəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>发光的，明亮的</td><td>a luminous dial<br>夜光表盘</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The luminous dial of his watch glowed faintly in the dark.  他手表的夜光表盘在黑暗中微微发亮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lustrous</td><td>/ˈlʌstrəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有光泽的</td><td>lustrous black hair<br>有光泽的黑发</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her lustrous black hair shone under the bright lights of the stage.  她有光泽的黑发在明亮的舞台灯光下闪耀。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 radiant</td><td>/ˈreɪdiənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>光芒四射的；喜气洋洋的</td><td>a radiant smile<br>灿烂的笑容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bride looked radiant as she walked into the garden.  新娘走进花园时神采飞扬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 refulgent</del></td><td>/rɪˈfʌldʒənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>辉煌的，灿烂的</td><td>refulgent golden domes<br>灿烂的金色圆顶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Refulgent golden domes rose above the rooftops of the old city.  灿烂的金色圆顶耸立在老城的屋顶之上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shining</td><td>/ˈʃaɪnɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>闪亮的，发光的</td><td>shining armor<br>闪亮的铠甲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her shining eyes showed how much the praise meant to her.  她发亮的眼睛显示出这份赞扬对她多重要。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ardent</td><td>/ˈɑːrdənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>热情的，热切的</td><td>an ardent supporter<br>热情的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He remained an ardent fan of the team for fifty years.  他五十年来一直是这支球队的狂热球迷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 demonstrative</td><td>/dɪˈmɑːnstrətɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>感情外露的</td><td>a demonstrative greeting<br>热情外露的问候</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her demonstrative hugs surprised colleagues used to formal handshakes.  她热情的拥抱让习惯正式握手的同事很惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 emotional</td><td>/ɪˈmoʊʃənəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>感情丰富的；动情的</td><td>an emotional farewell speech<br>动情的告别演讲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He became emotional when describing his father's last letter.  描述父亲最后一封信时他动了感情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fervid</td><td>/ˈfɜːrvɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>酷热的；热情的</td><td>a fervid supporter<br>热情的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His fervid speech moved even the quietest people in the room.  他热情洋溢的演讲连房间里最安静的人都被感动了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impassioned</td><td>/ɪmˈpæʃənd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>慷慨激昂的，充满激情的</td><td>an impassioned speech<br>慷慨激昂的演讲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave an impassioned defense of public libraries.  她慷慨激昂地为公共图书馆辩护。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 passionate</td><td>/ˈpæʃənət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>热情的，充满激情的</td><td>a passionate defense of art<br>对艺术的热情辩护</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is passionate about protecting the city's old neighborhoods.  她对保护城市的老街区充满热情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 torrid</td><td>/ˈtɔːrɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>酷热的；热情的，情感深厚的</td><td>the torrid zone<br>热带地区</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The torrid afternoon heat kept everyone indoors until the sun finally set.  酷热的午后让所有人待在室内，直到太阳终于落下。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vehement</td><td>/ˈviːəmənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（情感）强烈的，热情的</td><td>vehement opposition<br>强烈的反对</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She made a vehement protest against the new tax.  她对新税提出了强烈的抗议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dim</td><td>/ˈdɪm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>暗淡的，模糊的</td><td>a dim light<br>昏暗的灯光</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The room was too dim for reading anything but large print.  房间太暗，只能看大字。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dull</td><td>/dʌl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>迟钝的；乏味的</td><td>a dull student<br>迟钝的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lecture was so dull that half the class slept.  讲座乏味得让一半学生睡着了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lackluster</td><td>/ˈlæklʌstər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>黯淡无光泽的</td><td>a lackluster performance<br>平淡无光的表现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her lackluster hair and tired face worried everyone at home.  她黯淡的头发和疲惫的脸让家人都担心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cold</td><td>/ˈkoʊld/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>寒冷的；冷淡的</td><td>a cold morning<br>寒冷的早晨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The water was much too cold for anyone to swim.  水太冷了，没人能游泳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cool</td><td>/ˈkuːl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>凉爽的；冷淡的</td><td>a cool reception<br>冷淡的接待</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His proposal met a cool response from the committee.  他的提案得到委员会冷淡的回应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dispassionate</td><td>/dɪsˈpæʃənət/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷静客观的，不带感情的</td><td>a dispassionate analysis<br>冷静客观的分析</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge gave a dispassionate summary of both arguments.  法官冷静客观地总结了双方的论点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 emotionless</td><td>/ɪˈmoʊʃənləs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无感情的，冷漠的</td><td>an emotionless voice<br>毫无感情的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He read the verdict in a flat, emotionless tone.  他用平板毫无感情的语调宣读了判决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impassive</td><td>/ɪmˈpæsɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷漠的，无感情的</td><td>remain impassive under pressure<br>压力下仍无动于衷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His face stayed impassive as the verdict was read aloud.  宣读判决时，他的脸上始终毫无表情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unemotional</td><td>/ˌʌnɪˈmoʊʃənəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不动感情的，冷静的</td><td>an unemotional account<br>不带感情的叙述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her unemotional summary of the disaster unsettled the listeners.  她不带感情的灾难总结让听众不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>incantation</b></td><td>/ˌɪnkænˈteɪʃn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>咒语</td><td>chant an incantation<br>念咒语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The healer chanted an incantation over the sick child all night.  巫医整夜在病童身旁吟诵咒语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abracadabra</td><td>/ˌæbrəkəˈdæbrə/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>咒语；胡言乱语</td><td>mutter abracadabra<br>念念咒语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The magician waved his wand and shouted abracadabra.  魔术师挥动魔杖，喊出咒语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 bewitchment</del></td><td>/bɪˈwɪtʃmənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>施魔法，着魔</td><td>under his bewitchment<br>在他的魔法之下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The villagers blamed the illness on some bewitchment.  村民们把这场病归咎于某种魔法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 charm</td><td>/ˈtʃɑːrm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>迷住，吸引；魅力</td><td>charm the audience<br>迷住观众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young pianist charmed the audience with her first piece.  这位年轻钢琴家用第一首曲子迷住了观众。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conjuration</td><td>/ˌkɑndʒəˈreɪʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>恳求，祈求；咒语</td><td>a solemn conjuration<br>郑重的恳求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The letter closed with a solemn conjuration to keep the family secret.  这封信以郑重恳求保守家族秘密作结。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enchantment</td><td>/enˈtʃæntmənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>魔法，咒语；魅力</td><td>break the enchantment<br>解除魔法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only true courage could break the enchantment on the castle.  唯有真正的勇气才能解除城堡上的魔法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 glamour</td><td>/ˈɡlæmər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>魔法；魅力</td><td>the glamour of Hollywood<br>好莱坞的魅力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Old tales say a witch can cast a glamour over travelers.  古老传说称女巫能对旅人施下迷咒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hex</td><td>/ˈheks/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>咒语，恶咒；施咒</td><td>put a hex on him<br>对他施下恶咒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She joked that a hex had ruined her whole week.  她开玩笑说有个恶咒毁了她一整周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 invocation</td><td>/ˌɪnvəˈkeɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>咒语；祈求，祈祷</td><td>a magic invocation<br>一句魔法咒语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ceremony opened with a long invocation to the ancestors.  仪式以对祖先的长篇祈祷开始。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>incarnate</b></td><td>/ɪnˈkɑːrneɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>使（思想、理论）具化，体现</td><td>incarnate an ideal<br>体现一种理想</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Many people think Hitler incarnated pure evil and boundless self-regard.  许多人认为希特勒是纯粹邪恶与极端自我的化身。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 epitomize</td><td>/ɪˈpɪtəmaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>代表，体现，是…的典型范例</td><td>epitomize the era<br>是那个时代的典型</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 These narrow streets epitomize the era before automobiles reshaped the whole city.  这些狭窄的街道正是汽车重塑整座城市之前那个时代的典型。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incorporate</td><td>/ɪnˈkɔːrpəreɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>包含，纳入；体现</td><td>incorporate new ideas<br>纳入新想法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The revised design incorporates suggestions from every member of the team.  修订后的设计纳入了团队每位成员的建议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 manifest</td><td>/ˈmænɪfest/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>显然的，明显易懂的；显现，显露</td><td>manifest injustice<br>明显的不公</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His discomfort at the question was manifest to everyone seated around the table.  他对这个问题的不适让围坐桌边的每个人都看得明明白白。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 materialize</td><td>/məˈtɪriəˌlaɪz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>实现，成为现实；具体化</td><td>fail to materialize<br>未能实现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The promised funding never materialized, so the project was quietly dropped.  承诺的资金始终没到位，项目被悄悄放弃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 personalize</td><td>/ˈpɜːrsənəˌlaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使人格化，使具体化</td><td>personalize an abstract idea<br>使抽象观念人格化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poets often personalize the seasons as quarreling brothers.  诗人常把四季人格化为争吵的兄弟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 personify</td><td>/pərˈsɑːnəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>是…的化身，使人格化</td><td>personify justice<br>象征正义</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 For many readers, she personifies quiet courage.  在许多读者心中，她就是沉静勇气的化身。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 substantiate</td><td>/səbˈstænʃieɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实</td><td>substantiate the claim<br>证实这一说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No evidence has emerged to substantiate his serious accusations.  没有证据能证实他严重的指控。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 symbolize</td><td>/ˈsɪmbəˌlaɪz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>象征，代表</td><td>symbolize hope<br>象征希望</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The olive branch has long symbolized peace between enemies.  橄榄枝长久以来象征着敌人之间的和平。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 disembody</del></td><td>/ˌdɪsɪmˈbɑːdi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使脱离肉体</td><td>disembody the spirit<br>使灵魂脱离肉体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel imagines a machine that can disembody human memory.  这部小说设想出一台能使人类记忆脱离肉体的机器。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 2

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>incendiary</b></td><td>/ɪnˈsendieri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>煽动者；纵火者</td><td>a political incendiary<br>政治煽动者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The government labeled the anonymous pamphleteer a dangerous incendiary.  政府把这位匿名小册子作者称为危险的煽动者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 demagogue</td><td>/ˈdeməɡɑːɡ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>蛊惑民心的政客</td><td>a dangerous populist demagogue<br>危险的民粹政客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The demagogue blamed foreigners for every problem the province faced.  那个蛊惑民心的政客把全省的每个问题都归咎于外国人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exciter</td><td>/ɪkˈsaɪtər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>煽动者，激励者</td><td>an exciter of riots<br>骚乱的煽动者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pamphlet named him as the chief exciter of the mob.  这本小册子指他是这群暴民的主要煽动者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 firebrand</td><td>/ˈfaɪərˌbrænd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>煽动者，鼓动分子</td><td>a young political firebrand<br>年轻的政治煽动者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young firebrand stirred the crowd with furious speeches.  这个年轻的煽动者用愤怒的演讲激起人群。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 fomenter</del></td><td>/foʊˈmɛntər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>煽动者，挑起者</td><td>a fomenter of unrest<br>动乱的煽动者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Officials called him a fomenter of unrest in the province.  官员称他是该省动乱的煽动者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 inciter</del></td><td>/ɪnˈsaɪtər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>煽动者</td><td>the chief inciter<br>主要煽动者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police arrested the inciter of the street violence.  警方逮捕了这场街头暴力的煽动者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 instigator</td><td>/ˈɪnstəˌɡeɪtər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>煽动者，主使者</td><td>the instigator of the riot<br>骚乱的主使者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police identified the instigator of the riot from video footage.  警方从录像中认出了骚乱的主使者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 kindler</td><td>/ˈkɪndlər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>点燃者，煽动者</td><td>a kindler of rebellion<br>叛乱的点火者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 History remembers him as a kindler of popular rebellion.  历史记住他是民众叛乱的点火者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 provocateur</td><td>/prəˌvɑːkəˈtɜːr/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>挑动者，煽动分子</td><td>a political provocateur<br>政治挑动者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The blogger acted as a provocateur during the tense election.  这位博主在紧张的选举期间充当挑动者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 agitational</del></td><td>/ˌædʒɪˈteɪʃənl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>煽动性的</td><td>agitational propaganda<br>煽动性的宣传</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The newspaper was closed for printing agitational articles.  这家报纸因刊印煽动性文章而被查封。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 instigative</del></td><td>/ˈɪnstɪɡeɪtɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>煽动性的</td><td>instigative remarks<br>煽动性的言论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His instigative speech stirred the crowd into open revolt.  他煽动性的演讲激起人群公开反叛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 provocative</td><td>/proʊˈvɑːkətɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>煽动性的，挑拨的</td><td>a provocative speech<br>煽动性的演讲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The article was deliberately provocative to attract attention.  这篇文章故意挑拨，以吸引注意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 seditious</td><td>/sɪˈdɪʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>煽动性的，意在制造混乱的</td><td>seditious pamphlets<br>煽动性的小册子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soldiers found seditious pamphlets hidden beneath the floor of the barn.  士兵们在谷仓地板下发现了藏着的煽动性小册子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conciliatory</td><td>/kənˈsɪliəˌtɔːri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>安抚的，调和的</td><td>a conciliatory gesture<br>和解的姿态</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He adopted a conciliatory tone after the heated debate.  激烈辩论之后他改用了安抚的语气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pacific</td><td>/pəˈsɪfɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>平和的，爱好和平的</td><td>a pacific temperament<br>平和的性情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He settled the quarrel with a few pacific words.  他用几句平和的话平息了争吵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>incense</b></td><td>/ɪnˈsens/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激怒</td><td>incense the public<br>激怒公众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The proposal is certain to incense feminists across the country.  这项提案肯定会激怒全国的女权主义者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aggravate</td><td>/ˈæɡrəveɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加重，恶化；激怒</td><td>aggravate the situation<br>使局势恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Running on the injury will only aggravate it.  带着伤跑步只会让伤势加重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enrage</td><td>/enˈreɪdʒ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使愤怒，激怒</td><td>enrage the audience<br>激怒观众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His flippant apology only enraged the families further.  他轻率的道歉只让那些家庭更加愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exasperate</td><td>/ɪɡˈzæspəreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使恼怒，激怒</td><td>exasperate his parents<br>使他的父母恼火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His constant lateness exasperated even the most patient teachers.  他总是迟到，连最有耐心的老师都被激怒了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inflame</td><td>/ɪnˈfleɪm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使加剧</td><td>inflame public anger<br>使公众愤怒加剧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor's careless comments only inflamed public anger over the housing shortage.  市长轻率的言论只是加剧了公众对住房短缺的愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 infuriate</td><td>/ɪnˈfjʊrieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激怒</td><td>infuriate the critics<br>激怒批评者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The decision to cut pensions infuriated thousands of workers across the country.  削减养老金的决定激怒了全国成千上万的工人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 madden</td><td>/ˈmædən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使发狂，使愤怒</td><td>madden the audience<br>使观众暴怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The endless delays maddened passengers waiting at the gate.  无休止的延误让在登机口等待的乘客怒不可遏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 outrage</td><td>/ˈaʊˌtreɪdʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>愤怒；暴行；激怒</td><td>public outrage<br>公众的愤怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The verdict caused outrage among the victim's family and friends.  判决引起受害者家人和朋友的愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rankle</td><td>/ˈræŋkl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激怒</td><td>rankle in his mind<br>在他心里作痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The unfair remark continued to rankle him for many years afterward.  那句不公平的评论此后多年一直令他愤懑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rile</td><td>/raɪl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>刺激，惹怒</td><td>rile up the crowd<br>激怒人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing riles him more than being interrupted in the middle of a sentence.  没有什么比话说到一半被打断更让他恼火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 roil</td><td>/rɔɪl/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激怒</td><td>roil the crowd<br>激怒人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The careless remarks of the mayor roiled the crowd outside the courthouse.  市长轻率的言论激怒了法院外的人群。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 delight</td><td>/dɪˈlaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使高兴，使愉快；愉悦</td><td>delight the audience<br>使观众高兴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young pianist delighted the audience with a lively encore.  这位年轻钢琴家用一首活泼的加演曲取悦了观众。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gratify</td><td>/ˈɡrætɪfaɪ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使满足，使高兴</td><td>gratify a desire<br>满足某种欲望</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It gratified her to see her students succeed.  看到学生成功让她很欣慰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 please</td><td>/ˈpliːz/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>使高兴，使满意</td><td>please the crowd<br>取悦人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The simple ending pleased readers far more than critics expected.  这个简单的结局比评论家预料的更让读者满意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>inception</b></td><td>/ɪnˈsepʃn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>开端，开始</td><td>since its inception<br>自创立以来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The club has doubled in size since its inception three years ago.  这个俱乐部自三年前创立以来规模翻了一倍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 beginning</td><td>/bɪˈɡɪnɪŋ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>最初的，开端的；开始</td><td>the beginning stages<br>起始阶段</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We are still at the beginning of the investigation.  我们仍处于调查的开端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 birth</td><td>/ˈbɜːrθ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>诞生，开始；出生</td><td>the birth of an idea<br>一个想法的诞生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That meeting marked the birth of the modern computer industry.  那次会议标志着现代计算机产业的诞生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 commencement</td><td>/kəˈmensmənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>开始，开端；毕业典礼</td><td>the commencement of talks<br>谈判的开始</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The commencement of construction was delayed by heavy spring rains.  施工的开始因春季大雨而延后。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dawn</td><td>/ˈdɔːn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>开端；黎明</td><td>the dawn of civilization<br>文明的开端</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Printing marked the dawn of mass education in Europe.  印刷术标志着欧洲大众教育的开端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 genesis</td><td>/ˈdʒenəsɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>起源，创始</td><td>the genesis of the crisis<br>危机的起源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The genesis of the movement lies in a single student protest.  这场运动的起源是一次学生抗议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 kickoff</td><td>/ˈkɪkˌɔːf/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>开始，开端</td><td>the kickoff of the campaign<br>运动的开端</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The kickoff of the fundraising drive drew hundreds of donors.  募捐活动的启动吸引了数百名捐赠者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 launch</td><td>/ˈlɔːntʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>发起，开始；发射</td><td>launch a new campaign<br>发起新的活动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company plans to launch its cheapest model this autumn.  公司计划今秋推出其最便宜的型号。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 nascence</del></td><td>/ˈneɪsəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>诞生，发生，起源</td><td>the nascence of a movement<br>一场运动的诞生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Those pamphlets document the nascence of the labor movement.  那些小册子记录了工人运动的诞生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 onset</td><td>/ˈɑːnset/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>起始，开始</td><td>the onset of winter<br>冬天的开始</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The onset of winter caught the expedition without adequate shelter or fuel.  冬天的到来让探险队没有足够的庇护所和燃料。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 outset</td><td>/ˈaʊtset/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>开端，开始</td><td>from the outset<br>从一开始</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 From the outset, she made it clear that she would not compromise.  从一开始，她就明确表示自己不会妥协。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 start</td><td>/ˈstɑːrt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>开始，开端</td><td>from the start<br>从一开始</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 From the start it was clear the schedule was too tight.  从一开始就明显看出日程太紧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 threshold</td><td>/ˈθreʃˌhoʊld/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>开端；门槛，界限</td><td>on the threshold of change<br>处于变革的开端</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The country stands on the threshold of a major reform.  这个国家正处于一场重大改革的开端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 close</td><td>/kloʊz/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>关闭；结束</td><td>close the meeting<br>结束会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They closed the discussion before anyone could object.  他们在有人反对之前结束了讨论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conclusion</td><td>/kənˈkluːʒən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>结束；结论</td><td>bring to a conclusion<br>使结束</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The trial reached its conclusion after eleven long weeks.  审判在漫长的十一周后结束。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 end</td><td>/ˈend/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>结束；末端</td><td>end the war<br>结束战争</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The strike ended after both sides agreed on wages.  双方就工资达成一致后罢工结束。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 termination</td><td>/tərməˈneɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>终止，结束</td><td>the termination of the contract<br>合同的终止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Either side may request termination of the agreement early.  任何一方都可要求提前终止协议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 omega</td><td>/oʊˈmeɡə/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>结尾，最后</td><td>the alpha and omega<br>始与终</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 For him, that discovery was the omega of a lifelong search.  对他而言，那个发现是毕生求索的终点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>incessant</b></td><td>/ɪnˈsesnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无间断的</td><td>incessant noise<br>不间断的噪音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The incessant drumming from next door kept us awake all night.  隔壁不间断的鼓声让我们整夜无法入睡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ceaseless</td><td>/ˈsiːslɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不停的，持续的</td><td>ceaseless noise<br>不停的噪音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ceaseless traffic outside made it impossible for us to sleep.  外面不停的车流让我们无法入睡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 continual</td><td>/kənˈtɪnjuəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>连续的，不断的</td><td>continual interruptions<br>不断的打扰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Continual delays finally forced them to cancel the trip.  接连不断的延误最终迫使他们取消行程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nonstop</td><td>/ˌnɑːnˈstɑːp/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>不间断的；不停地</td><td>a nonstop flight<br>直飞的航班</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It rained nonstop for three days in the mountains.  山里连续下了三天不停的雨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perpetual</td><td>/pərˈpetʃuəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，不断的</td><td>perpetual motion<br>永恒运动，永动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He lives in perpetual fear of losing his job.  他一直生活在失业的恐惧中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 running</td><td>/ˈrʌnɪŋ/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>连续的，不断的</td><td>a running commentary<br>连续的解说</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They kept a running argument going for the whole journey.  他们一路上争论不休。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unbroken</td><td>/ənˈbroʊkən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不间断的，连续的</td><td>an unbroken silence<br>持续的沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team enjoyed an unbroken run of twelve victories.  这支队伍取得了连续十二场不间断的胜利。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unceasing</td><td>/ənˈsiːsɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不停的，持续的</td><td>unceasing effort<br>不懈的努力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her unceasing questions finally uncovered the truth.  她不停的追问最终揭开了真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uninterrupted</td><td>/ˌʌnɪntəˈrʌptɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不间断的，连续的</td><td>uninterrupted sleep<br>不间断的睡眠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He needs three uninterrupted hours to finish the draft.  他需要三小时不被打断的时间来完成草稿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unremitting</td><td>/ˌʌnrɪˈmɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>连续不断的</td><td>unremitting labor<br>不间断的劳作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her unremitting practice over ten years made her a fine cellist.  十年不间断的练习使她成为出色的大提琴手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 intermittent</td><td>/ˌɪntərˈmɪtənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>断断续续的</td><td>intermittent power failures<br>断断续续的停电</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Intermittent power failures made it impossible to finish the experiment that night.  断断续续的停电使当晚无法完成那项实验。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>inch</b></td><td>/ɪntʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）慢慢移动</td><td>inch forward slowly<br>缓慢向前挪动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The heavy truck had to inch forward slowly across the snowy bridge.  那辆重卡只能缓慢地挪过积雪覆盖的桥梁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crawl</td><td>/ˈkrɔːl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>缓慢行进；爬行</td><td>crawl along the highway<br>在公路上缓慢行进</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Traffic crawled along the flooded highway for nearly three hours.  车流在被淹的公路上缓慢行进了近三个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 creak</td><td>/ˈkriːk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>缓慢挪动；发出咯吱声</td><td>creak along the road<br>沿路缓慢挪行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old cart creaked slowly up the muddy hill.  那辆旧车吱吱作响地缓慢爬上泥泞的山坡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 creep</td><td>/kriːp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>缓慢地行进</td><td>creep along slowly<br>缓慢地爬行前进</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Traffic crept forward only a few meters at a time all morning.  整个上午车流每次只缓慢前进几米。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 limp</td><td>/lɪmp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、形容词</td><td>跛行，艰难缓慢地走；柔软的</td><td>limp toward the door<br>跛行走向门口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He limped slowly toward the door with his ankle wrapped in bandages.  他脚踝缠着绷带，一步一跛地慢慢走向门口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 plod</td><td>/ˈplɑːd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>沉重缓慢地走，苦干</td><td>plod through the mud<br>在泥地里沉重跋涉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tired horses plodded through the deep mud.  疲惫的马匹在深泥中沉重跋涉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slouch</td><td>/slaʊtʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>缓慢行走</td><td>slouch down the street<br>沿街缓慢走着</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The exhausted workers slouched slowly down the street toward the bus stop.  疲惫的工人沿街缓慢走向公交站。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 snail</td><td>/ˈsneɪl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>蜗牛；缓慢行进</td><td>move at a snail's pace<br>以蜗牛般的速度前进</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Traffic snailed along the flooded avenue all morning.  整个上午车流在被水淹的大道上蜗行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dart</td><td>/dɑːrt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>猛冲，突然移动</td><td>dart across the road<br>猛冲过马路</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A squirrel darted across the lawn and vanished up the old oak.  一只松鼠猛冲过草坪，蹿上老橡树不见了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fleet</td><td>/fliːt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迅速的</td><td>fleet of foot<br>脚步迅捷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fleet young messenger crossed the valley before dark.  这位迅捷的年轻信使天黑前穿过了山谷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flit</td><td>/flɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>轻快地飞过；掠过</td><td>flit from place to place<br>从一处快速掠到另一处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Small birds flit from branch to branch searching for hidden insects.  小鸟在枝间轻快地飞来飞去寻找隐藏的昆虫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 scurry</td><td>/ˈskɜːri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>急匆匆地跑</td><td>scurry for cover<br>急忙找掩护</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Mice scurried across the floor when the light came on.  灯一亮，老鼠们急匆匆跑过地板。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>inchoate</b></td><td>/ɪnˈkoʊət/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>新生的，才开始的</td><td>inchoate ideas<br>尚未成形的想法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His inchoate plans changed every time he described them to us.  他那些尚未成形的计划每次向我们描述时都变了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 aborning</del></td><td>/əˈbɔrnɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>刚萌芽的，正在诞生中的</td><td>die aborning<br>尚未成形便夭折</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reform movement died aborning when funding vanished.  资金消失后，改革运动尚未成形就夭折了。</td></tr>

<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dentist spotted incipient signs of decay in two of the teeth.  牙医发现两颗牙齿上有初现的蛀坏迹象。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 initial</td><td>/ˌɪˈnɪʃəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>最初的，开始的</td><td>the initial reaction<br>最初的反应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our initial estimate turned out to be far too low.  我们最初的估计结果远远偏低。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nascent</td><td>/ˈnæsnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>新生的</td><td>a nascent industry<br>新兴的行业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Government loans helped the nascent solar industry survive its first decade.  政府贷款帮助新兴的太阳能产业度过了最初十年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 adult</td><td>/əˈdʌlt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>成年的，成熟的；成年人</td><td>an adult attitude<br>成熟的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She handled the crisis in a calm, adult way.  她以冷静成熟的方式处理了这场危机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 full-blown</td><td>/ˌfʊlˈbloʊn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>完全成熟的，全面爆发的</td><td>a full-blown crisis<br>全面爆发的危机</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 full-fledged</td><td>/ˌfʊl ˈfledʒd/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>完全成熟的，羽翼已成的</td><td>a full-fledged member<br>正式成员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After three years she became a full-fledged engineer.  三年后她成为一名完全合格的工程师。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mature</td><td>/məˈtʃʊr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>成熟的；（使）成熟</td><td>a mature judgment<br>成熟的判断</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He seems remarkably mature for a boy of fifteen.  对一个十五岁男孩来说他显得格外成熟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ripe</td><td>/ˈraɪp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>成熟的，熟透的；时机成熟的</td><td>ripe for change<br>变革时机成熟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The peaches are finally ripe enough to pick and eat.  这些桃子终于熟到可以采下来吃了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>incinerate</b></td><td>/ɪnˈsɪnəreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>将…烧成灰烬</td><td>incinerate the waste<br>焚烧废弃物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city incinerates medical waste in a sealed furnace.  该市在密闭炉中焚烧医疗废物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 burn</td><td>/ˈbɜːrn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>燃烧，烧毁</td><td>burn the documents<br>烧毁文件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They burned the old letters in the garden that evening.  那天晚上他们在花园里烧掉了旧信。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 carbonize</del></td><td>/ˈkɑːrbəˌnaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使碳化，烧成炭</td><td>carbonize the wood<br>使木材碳化</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 douse</td><td>/daʊs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扑灭（火或灯）；浇透</td><td>douse the flames<br>扑灭火焰</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 extinguish</td><td>/ɪkˈstɪŋɡwɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>熄灭</td><td>extinguish the fire<br>扑灭大火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Firefighters worked all night to extinguish the burning warehouse.  消防员整夜工作扑灭燃烧的仓库。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 quench</td><td>/kwentʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解（渴）；熄灭</td><td>quench one's thirst<br>解渴</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳反 put out</td><td>/ˈpʊt ˈaʊt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使不便，麻烦；扑灭</td><td>put you out<br>麻烦到你</td></tr>
