<tr><td><b>incite</b></td><td>/ɪnˈsaɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>煽动，激起</td><td>incite a riot<br>煽动暴乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was charged with inciting a riot outside the county courthouse.  他被指控在县法院外煽动暴乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arouse</td><td>/əˈraʊz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>唤起，激起；唤醒</td><td>arouse public suspicion<br>引起公众怀疑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The missing files aroused suspicion among the auditors.  丢失的文件引起了审计人员的怀疑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abet</td><td>/əˈbet/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>帮助，教唆（做坏事）</td><td>aid and abet<br>帮助并教唆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was charged with aiding and abetting the robbery.  他被控协助并教唆抢劫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 foment</td><td>/foʊˈment/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>助长，煽动</td><td>foment unrest<br>煽动动乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pamphlets were designed to foment rebellion in the provinces.  这些小册子意在煽动各省叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 instigate</td><td>/ˈɪnstɪɡeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>煽动，激起</td><td>instigate a rebellion<br>煽动叛乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several officers were accused of helping to instigate the rebellion in the province.  几名军官被指控参与煽动该省的叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pick</td><td>/ˈpɪk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>挑起（争斗）；挑选</td><td>pick a fight<br>挑起争斗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He picked a quarrel with everyone at the table.  他跟桌上每个人都挑起了争吵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 provoke</td><td>/prəˈvoʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激怒；驱使，激起</td><td>provoke an angry response<br>引发愤怒的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His careless remark provoked an angry response from the whole audience.  他不慎的言论激起了全体观众的愤怒回应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 raise</td><td>/ˈreɪz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提出（问题）；举起，提高</td><td>raise an objection<br>提出反对意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He raised an objection just before the vote was taken.  就在投票前他提出了反对意见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stir</td><td>/ˈstɜːr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激起，煽动；搅拌</td><td>stir up anger<br>激起怒火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The article stirred up anger among local residents.  这篇文章激起了当地居民的愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dampen</td><td>/ˈdæmpən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抑制，压抑（感情、精力等），泼冷水</td><td>dampen his enthusiasm<br>打击他的热情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Losing the first game did nothing to dampen the team's spirits.  首场失利丝毫没有打击球队的士气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deter</td><td>/dɪˈtɜːr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>吓住，威慑</td><td>deter potential criminals<br>威慑潜在罪犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Better street lighting may deter thieves from working in this neighborhood.  更好的街道照明或许能威慑小偷不在这一带作案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 discourage</td><td>/dɪsˈkɜːrɪdʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使沮丧，使气馁；劝阻</td><td>discourage him from trying<br>劝他不要尝试</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Early failure should not discourage a young researcher.  早期的失败不该让年轻研究者气馁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dishearten</td><td>/dɪsˈhɑːrtn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使沮丧，使失去信心</td><td>disheartened by the news<br>因这消息而沮丧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The string of early losses disheartened even the most loyal fans.  开局连输让最忠实的球迷也失去了信心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dissuade</td><td>/dɪˈsweɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>劝阻，劝止</td><td>dissuade him from leaving<br>劝他不要离开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His parents tried to dissuade him from leaving school before the final term.  他父母试图劝他不要在最后一学期前退学。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>inclement</b></td><td>/ɪnˈklemənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（天气等）恶劣的</td><td>inclement weather<br>恶劣的天气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ceremony moved indoors because of unusually inclement weather.  由于天气异常恶劣，典礼改在室内举行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bleak</td><td>/ˈbliːk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（天气）阴冷的；荒凉的</td><td>a bleak winter day<br>阴冷的冬日</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The moor looked bleak under grey November skies.  灰暗的十一月天空下，荒原显得阴冷荒凉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 harsh</td><td>/hɑːrʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严厉的</td><td>harsh criticism<br>严厉的批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge's harsh sentence surprised even the prosecutors in the courtroom.  法官严厉的判决连法庭上的检察官都感到意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 severe</td><td>/səˈvɪr/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严厉的；严重的</td><td>a severe penalty<br>严厉的处罚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge imposed a severe penalty on the repeat offender.  法官对累犯施以严厉处罚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stormy</td><td>/ˈstɔːrmi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>暴风雨的；激烈的</td><td>a stormy meeting<br>激烈的会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The stormy meeting lasted until well after midnight.  这场激烈的会议一直开到午夜之后。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tempestuous</td><td>/temˈpestʃuəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>狂暴的，动荡不安的</td><td>a tempestuous relationship<br>剧烈动荡的关系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their tempestuous marriage ended after only two stormy years.  他们剧烈动荡的婚姻仅两年就结束了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bitter</td><td>/ˈbɪtər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严寒刺骨的；苦的</td><td>a bitter north wind<br>刺骨的北风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We waited an hour in bitter cold for the train.  我们在刺骨的严寒中等了一小时火车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brutal</td><td>/ˈbruːtəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>残忍的；直白无情的</td><td>a brutal attack<br>残忍的袭击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prisoners described the brutal treatment they received in the camp.  囚犯们描述了他们在营中受到的残忍对待。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intemperate</td><td>/ɪnˈtempərət/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无节制的，极端的，不温和的</td><td>intemperate language<br>过激的言辞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His intemperate language during the debate cost him the support of moderates.  他在辩论中过激的言辞使他失去了温和派的支持。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rigorous</td><td>/ˈrɪɡərəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（天气）严酷的；严格的</td><td>a rigorous climate<br>严酷的气候</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Few crops survive the rigorous winters of the plateau.  很少有作物能度过高原严酷的冬天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bright</td><td>/ˈbraɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>快活的，开朗的；明亮的</td><td>a bright smile<br>开朗的笑容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She greeted every customer with a bright and genuine smile.  她用开朗真诚的笑容迎接每位顾客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 clear</td><td>/ˈklɪr/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明确的，确凿的；清楚的</td><td>a clear majority<br>明显的多数</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party won by a clear majority of votes.  该党以明显多数票获胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cloudless</td><td>/ˈklaʊdləs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无云的，晴朗的</td><td>a cloudless blue sky<br>无云的蓝天</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We sailed all day beneath a cloudless summer sky.  我们在无云的夏日天空下航行了一整天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fair</td><td>/ˈfer/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>公正的；相当的；晴朗的</td><td>a fair trial<br>公正的审判</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Everyone deserves a fair hearing before any decision is made.  在做出任何决定之前，人人都应得到公正的申辩机会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sunny</td><td>/ˈsʌni/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>阳光充足的，晴朗的</td><td>a sunny afternoon<br>阳光明媚的下午</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children played outside on that sunny spring morning.  在那个阳光明媚的春日早晨，孩子们在外面玩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sunshiny</td><td>/ˈsʌnˌʃaɪni/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>阳光灿烂的</td><td>a sunshiny morning<br>阳光灿烂的早晨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was a sunshiny day, perfect for drying the laundry.  这是个阳光灿烂的日子，正适合晒衣服。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unclouded</td><td>/ʌnˈklaʊdɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无云的，晴朗的</td><td>an unclouded sky<br>晴朗的天空</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Stars were vivid in the unclouded desert night.  在晴朗的沙漠夜空中，星星格外明亮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 charitable</td><td>/ˈtʃærətəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽容的，仁慈的</td><td>a charitable view<br>宽容的看法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He took a charitable view of his student's careless mistake.  他对学生的粗心错误持宽容态度。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 clement</td><td>/ˈklemənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽容的，善良的；气候温和的</td><td>a clement judge<br>宽容的法官</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A clement judge gave the young offender a second chance.  一位宽容的法官给了这名年轻罪犯第二次机会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lenient</td><td>/ˈliːniənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽大仁慈的</td><td>a lenient sentence<br>从轻的判决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge handed down a lenient sentence because of the boy's age.  由于这个男孩年纪小，法官作出了从轻的判决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 merciful</td><td>/ˈmɜːrsɪfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>仁慈的，宽厚的</td><td>a merciful judge<br>宽仁的法官</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court was merciful and gave him only a small fine.  法庭很宽仁，只罚了他少许款项。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 3

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>incogitant</b></del></td><td>/ɪnˈkɑːdʒɪtənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>考虑不周的，不体谅的</td><td>an incogitant remark<br>欠考虑的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His incogitant comment about her illness hurt the whole family.  他关于她病情的那句欠考虑的话伤害了全家人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 discourteous</td><td>/dɪsˈkɜːrtiəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无礼的，粗鲁的</td><td>a discourteous reply<br>无礼的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was discourteous to leave without thanking the host.  不向主人道谢就离开是无礼的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disrespectful</td><td>/ˌdɪsrɪˈspektfəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不尊重的，无礼的</td><td>disrespectful behavior<br>不尊重人的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Interrupting your teacher like that is disrespectful.  那样打断老师说话是不尊重人的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ill-mannered</td><td>/ˌɪl ˈmænərd/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>举止粗鲁的，没礼貌的</td><td>an ill-mannered guest<br>没礼貌的客人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ill-mannered boy grabbed food before the guests sat down.  这个没礼貌的男孩在客人坐下前就抓起食物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impertinent</td><td>/ɪmˈpɜːrtnənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不相关的；无礼的，鲁莽的</td><td>an impertinent remark<br>无礼的言论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer objected that the question was impertinent to the charges.  律师反对说，这个问题与指控无关。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inconsiderate</td><td>/ˌɪnkənˈsɪdərət/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不体谅他人的</td><td>inconsiderate neighbors<br>不体谅人的邻居</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Playing loud music at midnight is simply inconsiderate.  半夜大声放音乐实在是不体谅别人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rude</td><td>/ˈruːd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗鲁的，无礼的</td><td>a rude remark<br>无礼的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was rude of him to leave without thanking the host.  他不谢主人就离开，很没礼貌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 thoughtless</td><td>/ˈθɔːtləs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>欠考虑的，轻率的</td><td>a thoughtless remark<br>轻率的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was thoughtless of him to slam the door at midnight.  他半夜摔门实在是欠考虑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ungracious</td><td>/ʌnˈɡreɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无礼的，不客气的</td><td>an ungracious refusal<br>不客气的拒绝</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It seemed ungracious to refuse such a generous offer.  拒绝如此大方的提议似乎显得无礼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 civil</td><td>/ˈsɪvəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>礼貌的；民事的</td><td>a civil reply<br>有礼的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Try to stay civil even when the discussion gets heated.  即使讨论变得激烈也要努力保持礼貌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 considerate</td><td>/kənˈsɪdərət/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>体谅的，考虑周到的</td><td>considerate of others<br>体谅他人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was considerate of her to call before visiting.  她来访前先打电话，很体贴周到。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 courteous</td><td>/ˈkɜːrtiəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>礼貌的，谦恭有礼的</td><td>a courteous reply<br>礼貌的答复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even when customers shouted, the young clerk remained calm and courteous.  即使顾客大声嚷嚷，那位年轻店员仍然平静而有礼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 genteel</td><td>/dʒenˈtiːl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有教养的，不粗俗的</td><td>genteel manners<br>有教养的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her genteel accent betrayed an expensive and very traditional upbringing.  她文雅的口音透露出昂贵而传统的成长背景。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gracious</td><td>/ˈɡreɪʃəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>和善的，亲切的</td><td>a gracious host<br>亲切的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was a gracious host who made every guest feel welcome.  她是位亲切的主人，让每位客人都感到受欢迎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 thoughtful</td><td>/ˈθɔːtfəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>深思熟虑的；体贴的</td><td>a thoughtful measured reply<br>深思熟虑的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave a thoughtful reply after considering the question carefully.  她仔细考虑问题后给出深思熟虑的回答。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>incongruent</b></td><td>/ɪnˈkɑːŋɡruənt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不全等的；不一致的</td><td>incongruent data sets<br>不一致的数据组</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two surveys produced incongruent results that nobody could explain.  两项调查得出了无人能解释的不一致结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conflicting</td><td>/kənˈflɪktɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冲突的，矛盾的</td><td>conflicting accounts<br>相互矛盾的说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Witnesses gave conflicting accounts of the driver's speed.  证人对司机车速的说法相互矛盾。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 discordant</td><td>/dɪˈskɔːrdənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不一致的，不和谐的</td><td>discordant opinions<br>不一致的意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Discordant voices from the back ruined the quiet ceremony.  后面不和谐的声音破坏了安静的仪式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 discrepant</td><td>/dɪˈskrɛpənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有差异的，不一致的</td><td>discrepant figures<br>互不相符的数字</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two audits produced discrepant figures for the same quarter.  两次审计对同一季度得出不一致的数字。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dissonant</td><td>/ˈdɪsənənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不和谐的，不一致的</td><td>dissonant voices<br>不和谐的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A dissonant chord broke the calm mood of the piece.  一个不和谐的和弦打破了乐曲平静的气氛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incompatible</td><td>/ˌɪnkəmˈpætəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不相容的，矛盾的</td><td>incompatible goals<br>互不相容的目标</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rapid growth and strict conservation seemed incompatible goals here.  在这里快速发展与严格保护似乎是矛盾的目标。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incongruous</td><td>/ɪnˈkɑːŋɡruəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不协调的，不相称的</td><td>an incongruous mixture<br>不协调的混合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His loud tie looked incongruous with the somber gray suit.  他花哨的领带与深灰西装很不协调。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 inconsonant</del></td><td>/ɪnˈkɑːnsənənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不一致的，不协调的</td><td>inconsonant with the facts<br>与事实不符</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His account is inconsonant with what the witnesses reported.  他的说法与证人所述不一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 congruent</td><td>/ˈkɑːŋɡruənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>和谐一致的；全等的</td><td>congruent with the facts<br>与事实相符</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His account is congruent with the facts we already know.  他的说法与我们已知的事实相符。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 congruous</td><td>/ˈkɑŋɡruəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>一致的，适合的，协调的</td><td>congruous with the setting<br>与环境协调</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plain wooden benches were congruous with the simple chapel.  素木长凳与这座朴素的礼拜堂很协调。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 consistent</td><td>/kənˈsɪstənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>一致的；始终如一的</td><td>consistent with the evidence<br>与证据相符</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His account is consistent with what the neighbors reported.  他的说法与邻居们所述一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>inconsequential</b></td><td>/ˌɪnkɑːnsɪˈkwenʃəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不重要的，微不足道的</td><td>an inconsequential remark<br>无关紧要的评论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The difference between the two prices is quite inconsequential.  两个价格之间的差别微不足道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fiddling</td><td>/ˈfɪdlɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>微不足道的，琐碎的</td><td>fiddling details<br>琐碎的细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not waste an afternoon on such fiddling corrections.  别为这些琐碎的修改浪费一个下午。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 frivolous</td><td>/ˈfrɪvələs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻浮的；无关紧要的</td><td>a frivolous remark<br>轻率无谓的言论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge dismissed the frivolous lawsuit within a single morning.  法官在一个上午之内就驳回了这起无理的诉讼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inconsiderable</td><td>/ˌɪnkənˈsɪdərəbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>微不足道的</td><td>an inconsiderable sum<br>微不足道的一笔钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The risk is small but by no means inconsiderable.  风险虽小，却绝非微不足道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insignificant</td><td>/ˌɪnsɪɡˈnɪfɪkənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无关紧要的，微不足道的</td><td>a seemingly insignificant change<br>看似微小的改动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An insignificant error early on ruined the entire calculation.  早期一个微不足道的错误毁了整个计算。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 minor</td><td>/ˈmaɪnər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>较小的，不重要的</td><td>a minor problem<br>一个小问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only minor repairs were needed after the small accident.  小事故后只需要一些小修理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 minute</td><td>/maɪˈnjuːt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极小的，微不足道的；细致的</td><td>a minute quantity<br>极小的量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even a minute trace of the chemical can spoil the batch.  即使极微量的这种化学品也会毁掉整批产品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 negligible</td><td>/ˈneɡlədʒəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>微不足道的，可忽略的</td><td>a negligible difference<br>可忽略的差异</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The extra cost is negligible compared with the savings.  与节省的钱相比，额外成本微不足道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 nugatory</del></td><td>/ˈnuːɡətɔːri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无关紧要的</td><td>a nugatory objection<br>无关紧要的反对</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court dismissed his nugatory objection and proceeded with the hearing.  法庭驳回了他无关紧要的反对，继续进行听证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 petty</td><td>/ˈpeti/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>细微的，不重要的</td><td>petty complaints<br>琐碎的抱怨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wasted the meeting on petty complaints about parking spaces.  他把会议浪费在关于停车位的琐碎抱怨上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slight</td><td>/slaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>轻微的，不重要的；轻视，怠慢</td><td>slight her contribution<br>轻视她的贡献</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He felt slighted when nobody mentioned his contribution during the ceremony.  典礼上无人提及他的贡献，他觉得受到了轻视。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trivial</td><td>/ˈtrɪviəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无足轻重的，不重要的</td><td>a trivial detail<br>无关紧要的细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor refused to argue over such a trivial difference in wording.  编辑不愿为措辞上如此无足轻重的差别争论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 consequential</td><td>/ˌkɑːnsəˈkwenʃəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>重要的；随之而来的</td><td>a consequential ruling<br>一项重要的裁决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That quiet vote proved the most consequential act of the session.  那次不起眼的投票成了本届会议最重要的举动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 eventful</td><td>/ɪˈventfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>多事的，重要的</td><td>an eventful year<br>多事的一年</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It has been an eventful year for the whole family.  对全家来说这是多事的一年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 important</td><td>/ˌɪmˈpɔːrtənt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>重要的，重大的</td><td>an important decision<br>一个重要的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sleep is as important as diet for a young athlete.  对年轻运动员来说，睡眠和饮食一样重要。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 meaningful</td><td>/ˈmiːnɪŋfəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有意义的，意味深长的</td><td>meaningful work<br>有意义的工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She left banking to find more meaningful work.  她离开银行业去寻找更有意义的工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 momentous</td><td>/moʊˈmentəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>重大的，重要的</td><td>a momentous decision<br>一个重大的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board reached a momentous decision that changed the company's direction entirely.  董事会做出了一个重大决定，彻底改变了公司的方向。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 significant</td><td>/səɡˈnɪfɪkənt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>重要的；显著的</td><td>a significant difference<br>显著的差别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers found a significant drop in the pollution levels.  研究者发现污染水平显著下降。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 substantial</td><td>/səbˈstænʃl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>大量的，可观的；实质的</td><td>a substantial increase<br>大幅的增长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company reported a substantial increase in profits this quarter.  公司报告本季度利润大幅增长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 weighty</td><td>/ˈweɪti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>重要的，重大的；沉重的</td><td>weighty matters<br>重大的事务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The elders debated weighty questions of land and water.  长者们讨论着土地与水源的重大问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>incontrovertible</b></td><td>/ˌɪnkɑːntrəˈvɜːrtəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无可争议的</td><td>incontrovertible facts<br>无可争议的事实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The DNA test provided incontrovertible evidence of his innocence.  DNA 检测提供了他无罪的无可争议的证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 certain</td><td>/ˈsɜːrtən/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>确定的，无疑的</td><td>certain of the outcome<br>对结果有把握</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We are certain that the letter was never sent.  我们确信那封信从未寄出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inarguable</td><td>/ɪnˈɑːrɡjuəbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无可争辩的</td><td>an inarguable truth<br>无可争辩的真理</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That the climate is warming is now inarguable.  气候正在变暖，这一点现在无可争辩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incontestable</td><td>/ˌɪnkənˈtestəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无可争辩的，不容置疑的</td><td>incontestable proof<br>无可争辩的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The photograph offered incontestable evidence of the meeting.  这张照片提供了会面的无可争辩的证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indisputable</td><td>/ˌɪndɪˈspjuːtəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无可争辩的</td><td>indisputable evidence<br>无可争辩的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The photographs offered indisputable proof of the damage.  这些照片提供了损害的无可争辩的证明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indubitable</td><td>/ɪnˈduːbɪtəbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不容置疑的</td><td>an indubitable fact<br>不容置疑的事实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her talent for languages is indubitable.  她的语言天赋是不容置疑的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 positive</td><td>/ˈpɑːzətɪv/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>确定的，明确的；积极的</td><td>positive proof<br>确凿的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I am positive that I locked the back door.  我确定我锁了后门。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sure</td><td>/ˈʃʊr/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>确定的，可靠的</td><td>a sure sign<br>可靠的迹象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Falling leaves are a sure sign that autumn has arrived.  落叶是秋天已到的可靠迹象。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 undeniable</td><td>/ˌʌndɪˈnaɪəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可否认的，明显的</td><td>undeniable talent<br>不可否认的才华</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her influence on modern dance is undeniable.  她对现代舞的影响不可否认。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unquestionable</td><td>/ənˈkwestʃənəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>毫无疑问的</td><td>unquestionable integrity<br>毋庸置疑的正直</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His loyalty to the team is unquestionable.  他对团队的忠诚毫无疑问。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 arguable</td><td>/ˈɑːrɡjuəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有争议的，可辩论的</td><td>an arguable point<br>有争议的一点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Whether the reform helped the poor is highly arguable.  这项改革是否帮助了穷人很有争议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 controversial</td><td>/ˌkɑːntrəˈvɜːrʃəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有争议的，引起争论的</td><td>a controversial decision<br>有争议的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge's controversial ruling was debated for months afterward.  法官那项有争议的裁决之后被讨论了数月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 debatable</td><td>/dəˈbeɪtəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有争议的，可商议的</td><td>a debatable assumption<br>有争议的假设</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Whether the policy helped the poor is debatable.  该政策是否帮助了穷人尚有争议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disputable</td><td>/dɪˈspjuːtəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有争议的，可质疑的</td><td>a disputable conclusion<br>可质疑的结论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His figures are accurate but his conclusion is disputable.  他的数字准确，但结论值得质疑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 problematic</td><td>/ˌprɑːbləˈmætɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有问题的，成问题的</td><td>a problematic assumption<br>有问题的假设</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The data set is problematic because many records are missing.  该数据集有问题，因为许多记录缺失。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 questionable</td><td>/ˈkwestʃənəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可疑的，靠不住的</td><td>questionable evidence<br>可疑的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The deal rested on questionable accounting practices.  这笔交易建立在可疑的会计做法上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 refutable</td><td>/rɪˈfjuːtəbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可驳倒的，可反驳的</td><td>a refutable claim<br>可以反驳的主张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A scientific theory must be refutable by observation.  科学理论必须能被观察所反驳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>incorrigible</b></td><td>/ɪnˈkɔːrɪdʒəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>屡教不改的；无法矫正的</td><td>an incorrigible liar<br>屡教不改的骗子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Everyone knew he was an incorrigible liar, yet they still listened.  大家都知道他是个屡教不改的骗子，却还是听他讲。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incurable</td><td>/ɪnˈkjʊrəbl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可治愈的；难以改变的</td><td>an incurable disease<br>不可治愈的疾病</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is an incurable optimist, even after losing everything.  即便失去一切，他仍是个改不了的乐观主义者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 irrecoverable</td><td>/ˌɪrɪˈkʌvərəbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无法挽回的，不能收回的</td><td>an irrecoverable loss<br>无法挽回的损失</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The flood caused irrecoverable damage to the archive.  洪水对档案造成了无法挽回的损害。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 irredeemable</td><td>/ˌɪrɪˈdiːməbl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可救药的，无法挽救的</td><td>an irredeemable scoundrel<br>不可救药的恶棍</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The critic dismissed the film as irredeemable rubbish.  评论家把这部电影贬为无可救药的垃圾。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 irremediable</td><td>/ˌɪrɪˈmiːdiəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无法补救的，不可挽回的</td><td>an irremediable mistake<br>无法补救的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 By then the split in the party was irremediable.  到那时党内的分裂已无法补救。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 irretrievable</td><td>/ˌɪrɪˈtriːvəbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可挽回的，无法找回的</td><td>an irretrievable breakdown<br>无法挽回的破裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Deleting the file caused an irretrievable loss of data.  删除该文件导致数据无法找回。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unrecoverable</td><td>/ˌʌnrɪˈkʌvərəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无法恢复的，不能收回的</td><td>unrecoverable data<br>无法恢复的数据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The engineers declared the crashed drive unrecoverable.  工程师宣布损坏的硬盘无法恢复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 unredeemable</del></td><td>/ˌʌnrɪˈdiːməbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可救赎的，无法挽救的</td><td>an unredeemable failure<br>无法挽救的失败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He refused to see any person as wholly unredeemable.  他不肯把任何人看作完全不可救赎的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 headstrong</td><td>/ˈhedˌstrɔːŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>任性的，固执的</td><td>a headstrong child<br>任性的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The headstrong young man ignored every warning from his parents.  这个任性的年轻人对父母的每句劝告都置之不理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intractable</td><td>/ˌɪnˈtræktəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难对付的，棘手的</td><td>an intractable problem<br>棘手的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poverty remains an intractable problem in many rural regions.  贫困在许多农村地区仍是棘手的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obstinate</td><td>/ˈɑːbstɪnət/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的</td><td>an obstinate refusal<br>固执的拒绝</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her obstinate refusal to compromise delayed the agreement by several months.  她固执地拒绝妥协，使协议推迟了几个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 refractory</td><td>/rɪˈfræktəri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>倔强的，不顺从的</td><td>a refractory pupil<br>不顺从的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One refractory pupil disrupted the lesson for the entire class.  一个不顺从的学生扰乱了全班的课。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stubborn</td><td>/ˈstʌbərn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的，顽固的</td><td>as stubborn as a mule<br>像骡子一样固执</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My grandfather was too stubborn to see a doctor.  我祖父太固执，不肯去看医生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unmanageable</td><td>/ənˈmænɪdʒəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以管理的，难控制的</td><td>unmanageable debt<br>难以控制的债务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The class grew unmanageable after the substitute arrived.  代课老师来后，这个班变得难以管理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unruly</td><td>/ʌnˈruːli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难驾驭的，不守规矩的</td><td>an unruly crowd<br>不守规矩的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two teachers struggled to calm the unruly class.  两位老师费力地安抚那个不守规矩的班级。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 willful</td><td>/ˈwɪlfl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的，倔强的</td><td>willful disobedience<br>故意的违抗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His willful refusal to ask for help delayed the repair by weeks.  他固执地拒绝求助，使维修推迟了好几周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 curable</td><td>/ˈkjʊrəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可治愈的</td><td>a curable condition<br>可治愈的病症</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Detected early, this cancer is often curable.  若早期发现，这种癌症通常可以治愈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 reclaimable</del></td><td>/rɪˈkleɪməbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可挽回的，可回收的</td><td>reclaimable land<br>可开垦的土地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Much of the plastic waste is still reclaimable.  这些塑料垃圾中很多仍可回收利用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 recoverable</td><td>/rɪˈkʌvərəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可恢复的，可收回的</td><td>recoverable losses<br>可弥补的损失</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only part of the investment proved recoverable.  这笔投资只有一部分被证明可以收回。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 redeemable</td><td>/rɪˈdiːməbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可赎回的；可挽救的</td><td>redeemable coupons<br>可兑换的优惠券</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 These points are redeemable for flights or hotel nights.  这些积分可兑换机票或酒店住宿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 remediable</del></td><td>/rɪˈmiːdiəbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可补救的</td><td>a remediable defect<br>可以补救的缺陷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most of the problems in the draft are easily remediable.  草稿里大多数问题都很容易补救。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 retrievable</td><td>/rɪˈtriːvəbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可取回的，可挽回的</td><td>retrievable files<br>可恢复的文件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Backups keep the records retrievable for ten years.  备份使这些记录在十年内都可以取回。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 savable</del></td><td>/ˈseɪvəbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可挽救的</td><td>a savable patient<br>有救的病人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The doctors argued about whether the leg was savable.  医生们争论那条腿是否还能保住。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 compliant</td><td>/kəmˈplaɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的</td><td>a compliant employee<br>顺从的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children were unusually compliant during the long ceremony.  在漫长的仪式上，孩子们出奇地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 docile</td><td>/ˈdɑːsəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>温顺的，听话的</td><td>a docile animal<br>温顺的动物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old horse was docile enough for beginners to ride.  这匹老马很温顺，初学者也能骑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 manageable</td><td>/ˈmænɪdʒəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易管理的，好对付的</td><td>a manageable workload<br>可应付的工作量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Split the task into smaller, manageable pieces first.  先把任务拆成较小、好处理的部分。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obedient</td><td>/əˈbiːdiənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>服从的，顺从的</td><td>an obedient child<br>听话的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dog sat quietly, obedient to every hand signal.  那只狗安静地坐着，服从每个手势。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 submissive</td><td>/səbˈmɪsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>服从的，顺从的</td><td>a submissive attitude<br>顺从的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke in a submissive tone that surprised her colleagues.  她以顺从的语气说话，令同事惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tractable</td><td>/ˈtræktəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易驾驭的，温顺的</td><td>a tractable student<br>听话的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The puppy proved surprisingly tractable during the first weeks of training.  这只小狗在最初几周的训练中出乎意料地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>incriminate</b></td><td>/ɪnˈkrɪmɪneɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>归罪于</td><td>incriminate oneself<br>使自己受牵连</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He refused to answer questions that might incriminate him.  他拒绝回答可能使自己入罪的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 charge</td><td>/ˈtʃɑːrdʒ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>指控；费用；充电</td><td>face criminal charges<br>面临刑事指控</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prosecutor charged him with fraud after months of investigation.  经过数月调查，检察官指控他欺诈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 criminate</del></td><td>/ˈkrɪməneɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>指控，归罪于</td><td>criminate an accomplice<br>指控一名同伙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His own letters criminated him at the trial.  他自己的信件在审判中给他定了罪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impeach</td><td>/ɪmˈpiːtʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>控告，弹劾；质疑</td><td>impeach the president<br>弹劾总统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer tried to impeach the witness's credibility.  律师试图质疑证人的可信度。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indict</td><td>/ɪnˈdaɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>起诉，控告</td><td>indict him for bribery<br>起诉他受贿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A grand jury indicted the mayor for taking bribes last year.  大陪审团去年起诉了这位市长受贿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 absolve</td><td>/əbˈzɑːlv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>使无罪，解除责任</td><td>absolve someone of blame<br>免除某人的责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report absolved the pilot of all blame.  报告免除了飞行员的全部责任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 acquit</td><td>/əˈkwɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>宣判无罪</td><td>acquit of murder<br>宣判谋杀罪不成立</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The jury acquitted him of all charges.  陪审团宣判他所有罪名不成立。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exculpate</td><td>/ˈekskʌlpeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>声明无罪；开脱，使无罪</td><td>exculpate the accused<br>为被告开脱罪责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New evidence exculpated the accused man after ten years in prison.  新证据在这名被告入狱十年后为他开脱了罪责。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exonerate</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɑːnəreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>免除责备</td><td>exonerate from blame<br>免除责备</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New evidence exonerated the wrongly jailed man.  新证据为这名被误判入狱者洗清了罪名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vindicate</td><td>/ˈvɪndɪkeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>为…平反，为…辩护，使无罪；证明，证实</td><td>vindicate one's reputation<br>为自己的名誉洗冤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The results vindicated her unusual approach.  结果证明了她那不寻常的方法是对的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>incubate</b></td><td>/ˈɪŋkjubeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>孵化；帮助，培养，促进</td><td>incubate a startup<br>孵化一家初创公司</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The university incubates dozens of small companies every single year.  这所大学每年孵化数十家小公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brood</td><td>/ˈbruːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>孵蛋；沉思</td><td>brood over her eggs<br>伏在蛋上孵化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hen brooded patiently over her eggs for weeks.  母鸡耐心地伏在蛋上孵了几周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hatch</td><td>/ˈhætʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>孵化，孵出</td><td>hatch the eggs<br>孵蛋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The eggs hatched three days earlier than expected.  这些蛋比预期早三天孵化了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sit</td><td>/ˈsɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>孵蛋；坐</td><td>sit on the eggs<br>伏在蛋上孵化</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 advance</td><td>/ədˈvæns/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>前进；促进；预付款</td><td>advance a theory<br>提出一种理论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The army advanced slowly through the thick forest at dawn.  黎明时军队缓慢穿过密林前进。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cultivate</td><td>/ˈkʌltɪveɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提升，加强；种植，培养</td><td>cultivate a friendship<br>培养一段友谊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She cultivated a wide network of contacts during her years abroad.  她在国外的岁月里培养了广泛的人际关系网。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 encourage</td><td>/ɪnˈkɜːrɪdʒ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>鼓励，激励；促进</td><td>encourage students to read<br>鼓励学生阅读</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 forward</td><td>/ˈfɔːrwərd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>向前的；前部的</td><td>take a forward step<br>向前迈一步</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He leaned forward to hear the quiet speaker better.  他向前倾身以便听清声音轻的讲话者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 further</td><td>/ˈfɜːrðər/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>促进，推动；更远的</td><td>further his career<br>促进他的事业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She took the job abroad to further her research on glaciers.  她接受海外工作以推进她的冰川研究。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nourish</td><td>/ˈnɜːrɪʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>培养，促进</td><td>nourish young talent<br>培养年轻人才</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The foundation exists to nourish young talent in music and the visual arts.  这家基金会的存在是为了培养音乐和视觉艺术方面的年轻人才。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nurse</td><td>/ˈnɜːrs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>培育，照料；护士</td><td>nurse a grudge<br>心怀怨恨</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nurture</td><td>/ˈnɜːrtʃər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>培育，培养</td><td>nurture young talent<br>培养年轻人才</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 discourage</td><td>/dɪsˈkɜːrɪdʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使沮丧，使气馁；劝阻</td><td>discourage him from trying<br>劝他不要尝试</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 foray</td><td>/ˈfɔːreɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>突袭，入侵；初次尝试</td><td>a foray into enemy territory<br>突入敌方领土</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cavalry made a brief foray across the frozen river.  骑兵越过冰封的河流做了一次短暂突袭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inroad</td><td>/ˈɪnˌroʊd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>侵袭，侵入</td><td>make inroads into the region<br>侵入该地区</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Raiders made repeated inroads into the border villages.  袭击者一再侵入边境村庄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 invasion</td><td>/ɪnˈveɪʒən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>入侵，侵略</td><td>a full-scale invasion<br>全面入侵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The invasion began before dawn on a foggy morning.  入侵在一个雾气弥漫的清晨黎明前开始。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 irruption</td><td>/ɪˈrʌpʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>侵入，突然闯入</td><td>an irruption of troops<br>军队的突然侵入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sudden irruption of soldiers silenced the market square.  士兵的突然闯入使集市广场安静下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 raid</td><td>/ˈreɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>突袭，袭击</td><td>an air raid<br>一次空袭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soldiers raided the camp shortly after midnight.  士兵在午夜过后不久突袭了营地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 retreat</td><td>/riˈtriːt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>避难所，隐居处</td><td>a mountain retreat<br>山间隐居处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cabin became his retreat from the noise of the city.  那间小屋成了他躲避城市喧闹的避难所。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 withdrawal</td><td>/wɪðˈdrɔːəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>撤退，撤离</td><td>the withdrawal of troops<br>军队的撤离</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treaty required the withdrawal of all foreign forces.  条约要求所有外国部队撤离。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>indelible</b></td><td>/ɪnˈdeləbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无法忘怀的</td><td>an indelible impression<br>难以忘怀的印象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The summer she spent in Kyoto left an indelible impression on her memory.  在京都度过的那个夏天在她记忆中留下了难以忘怀的印象。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impressive</td><td>/ɪmˈpresɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人印象深刻的</td><td>an impressive performance<br>令人印象深刻的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her command of four languages is genuinely impressive.  她掌握四门语言的能力确实令人印象深刻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 memorable</td><td>/ˈmemərəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难忘的，值得纪念的</td><td>a memorable evening<br>一个难忘的夜晚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The final match was memorable for its dramatic ending.  那场决赛因戏剧性的结局而令人难忘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unforgettable</td><td>/ˌʌnfərˈɡetəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以忘怀的</td><td>an unforgettable journey<br>一次难忘的旅程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The view from the summit was simply unforgettable.  从峰顶看到的景色实在令人难忘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 forgettable</td><td>/fɔrˈɡetəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>容易被忘记的</td><td>a forgettable film<br>一部记不住的电影</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel was pleasant but entirely forgettable.  这部小说读来愉快，却毫无记忆点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 4

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>indemnity</b></td><td>/ɪnˈdemnəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（损害、伤害等的）保险补偿</td><td>claim an indemnity<br>索取保险赔偿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The insurance company paid a generous indemnity for the damage caused by the flood.  保险公司为洪水造成的损失支付了一笔丰厚的赔偿金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compensation</td><td>/ˌkɑːmpənˈseɪʃən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>补偿，赔偿金</td><td>claim compensation for damage<br>索取损害赔偿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Workers received compensation for the injuries they suffered.  工人们获得了所受伤害的赔偿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indemnification</td><td>/ˌɪndemnəfɪˈkeɪʃən/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>赔偿，补偿</td><td>full indemnification for losses<br>损失的全额赔偿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract promises indemnification against any legal costs.  合同承诺赔偿一切法律费用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 quittance</del></td><td>/ˈkwɪtəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>债务免除；补偿，报答</td><td>a quittance for the debt<br>债务的免除凭据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The merchant signed a quittance for the remaining sum.  商人为余款签署了一份免除凭据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recompense</td><td>/ˈrekəmˌpens/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>补偿，报酬</td><td>recompense for the loss<br>对损失的补偿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was given a small sum as recompense for her trouble.  她得到一小笔钱作为麻烦的补偿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 redress</td><td>/rɪˈdres/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>赔偿，纠正</td><td>seek legal redress<br>寻求法律赔偿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Victims went to court to seek redress for their losses.  受害者诉诸法庭为损失寻求赔偿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 remuneration</td><td>/rɪˌmjuːnəˈreɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>报酬，酬金</td><td>generous remuneration<br>丰厚的报酬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He accepted the post despite the modest remuneration.  尽管报酬不高，他还是接受了这个职位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reparation</td><td>/ˌrepəˈreɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>赔偿，补偿</td><td>pay war reparations<br>支付战争赔款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The defeated state was forced to pay heavy reparations.  战败国被迫支付巨额赔款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 requital</td><td>/rɪˈkwaɪtəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>报答，补偿；报复</td><td>requital for his kindness<br>对他好意的报答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She sent a gift in requital for their long hospitality.  她送去礼物以报答他们长久的款待。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>indict</b></td><td>/ɪnˈdaɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>起诉，控告</td><td>indict him for bribery<br>起诉他受贿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A grand jury indicted the mayor for taking bribes last year.  大陪审团去年起诉了这位市长受贿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 charge</td><td>/ˈtʃɑːrdʒ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>指控；费用；充电</td><td>face criminal charges<br>面临刑事指控</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prosecutor charged him with fraud after months of investigation.  经过数月调查，检察官指控他欺诈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 criminate</del></td><td>/ˈkrɪməneɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>指控，归罪于</td><td>criminate an accomplice<br>指控一名同伙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His own letters criminated him at the trial.  他自己的信件在审判中给他定了罪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impeach</td><td>/ɪmˈpiːtʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>控告，弹劾；质疑</td><td>impeach the president<br>弹劾总统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer tried to impeach the witness's credibility.  律师试图质疑证人的可信度。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incriminate</td><td>/ɪnˈkrɪmɪneɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>归罪于</td><td>incriminate oneself<br>使自己受牵连</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He refused to answer questions that might incriminate him.  他拒绝回答可能使自己入罪的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 absolve</td><td>/əbˈzɑːlv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>使无罪，解除责任</td><td>absolve someone of blame<br>免除某人的责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report absolved the pilot of all blame.  报告免除了飞行员的全部责任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 acquit</td><td>/əˈkwɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>宣判无罪</td><td>acquit of murder<br>宣判谋杀罪不成立</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The jury acquitted him of all charges.  陪审团宣判他所有罪名不成立。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exculpate</td><td>/ˈekskʌlpeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>声明无罪；开脱，使无罪</td><td>exculpate the accused<br>为被告开脱罪责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New evidence exculpated the accused man after ten years in prison.  新证据在这名被告入狱十年后为他开脱了罪责。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exonerate</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɑːnəreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>免除责备</td><td>exonerate from blame<br>免除责备</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New evidence exonerated the wrongly jailed man.  新证据为这名被误判入狱者洗清了罪名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vindicate</td><td>/ˈvɪndɪkeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>为…平反，为…辩护，使无罪；证明，证实</td><td>vindicate one's reputation<br>为自己的名誉洗冤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The results vindicated her unusual approach.  结果证明了她那不寻常的方法是对的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>indifferent</b></td><td>/ɪnˈdɪfrənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不感兴趣的，冷漠的；平庸的</td><td>indifferent to criticism<br>对批评漠不关心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is entirely indifferent to what her critics say.  她对批评者说什么完全无所谓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aloof</td><td>/əˈluːf/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷淡的，冷漠孤傲的</td><td>remain aloof from<br>对…保持冷淡疏远</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stayed aloof from office gossip and rarely joined lunch.  他对办公室的闲话保持冷淡，很少参加午餐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 apathetic</td><td>/ˌæpəˈθetɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷漠的，无兴趣的</td><td>apathetic voters<br>冷漠的选民</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most students seemed apathetic about the election and did not vote.  大多数学生对这次选举漠不关心，也没有投票。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 detached</td><td>/dɪˈtætʃt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷漠超然的，不介入的</td><td>a detached attitude<br>超然冷漠的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She described the accident in a strangely detached voice.  她用一种异常冷漠的语气描述了那场事故。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incurious</td><td>/ɪnˈkjʊriəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不好奇的，漠不关心的</td><td>an incurious glance<br>漠不关心的一瞥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The guard was incurious about where we were going.  那名警卫对我们要去哪儿毫不好奇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nonchalant</td><td>/ˌnɑːnʃəˈlɑːnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>漫不经心的，淡然的</td><td>a nonchalant shrug<br>漫不经心地一耸肩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He answered the accusation with a nonchalant shrug and changed the subject.  他冷淡地一耸肩回应指控，然后换了话题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 numb</td><td>/nʌm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>麻木的，无感情的</td><td>numb with grief<br>因悲伤而麻木</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Numb with grief, she answered the reporters' questions in a flat voice.  她因悲伤而麻木，用平板的声音回答记者的提问。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 pococurante</del></td><td>/ˌpoʊkoʊkjʊˈrænti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>漠不关心的；漠不关心者</td><td>a pococurante attitude<br>漠不关心的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He listened with a pococurante air, saying nothing at all.  他带着漠不关心的神情听着，一言不发。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 remote</td><td>/rɪˈmoʊt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷淡的，疏离的；遥远的</td><td>a remote expression<br>疏离的神情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His manner remained remote even among close family members.  即使在近亲之间他也显得疏离。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unconcerned</td><td>/ˌʌnkənˈsɜːrnd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不关心的，漠不关心的</td><td>unconcerned about risk<br>对风险漠不关心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She seemed entirely unconcerned about the noise from the street.  她似乎完全不在意街上的噪音。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uninterested</td><td>/ʌnˈɪntrəstɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不感兴趣的，漠不关心的</td><td>uninterested in politics<br>对政治不感兴趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The students seemed uninterested in the long lecture.  学生们对这场长讲座似乎不感兴趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 attentive</td><td>/əˈtentɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>留心的，专注的；周到的</td><td>an attentive audience<br>专注的听众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nurse stayed attentive to every change in his breathing.  护士留心他呼吸的每一点变化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 concerned</td><td>/kənˈsɜːrnd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>关心的，担忧的</td><td>concerned about the outcome<br>关心结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Parents are deeply concerned about the new school policy.  家长们非常关心新的学校政策。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 interested</td><td>/ˈɪntrəstɪd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>感兴趣的</td><td>interested in the topic<br>对这个话题感兴趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She became interested in astronomy at the age of nine.  她九岁时开始对天文学产生兴趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>indigenous</b></td><td>/ɪnˈdɪdʒənəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>土产的，本地的；与生俱来的，先天的</td><td>indigenous plants of the region<br>该地区的本土植物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Many indigenous species of the island cannot survive anywhere else on the planet.  这个岛上许多本土物种无法在地球其他任何地方存活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aboriginal</td><td>/ˌæbəˈrɪdʒənəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>原住的，土著的</td><td>aboriginal languages of Taiwan<br>台湾的原住民语言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers recorded songs from several aboriginal communities.  研究者录下了几个原住民社区的歌谣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 endemic</td><td>/enˈdemɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>地方性的</td><td>endemic to this island<br>这个岛特有的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This flightless bird is endemic to a single island in the Pacific.  这种不会飞的鸟只见于太平洋上的一个岛屿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 native</td><td>/ˈneɪtɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>本地的，原产的；本地人</td><td>native to southern China<br>原产于华南</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This bamboo is native to the wet valleys of the south.  这种竹子原产于南方潮湿的山谷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 constitutional</td><td>/ˌkɑːnstəˈtuːʃənəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>体质上的，内在固有的</td><td>a constitutional weakness<br>体质上的虚弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His shyness seems constitutional rather than a habit he learned.  他的害羞似乎是天性使然，而非后天习得的习惯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 essential</td><td>/ɪˈsenʃəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>本质的，必不可少的</td><td>an essential quality<br>本质的特性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Patience is an essential part of her character.  耐心是她性格中本质的一部分。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 immanent</td><td>/ˈɪmənənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>内在的</td><td>immanent in nature<br>内在于自然之中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He argued that order is immanent in nature rather than imposed on it.  他认为秩序内在于自然，而非外加于自然。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inborn</td><td>/ˈɪnˌbɔːrn/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>天生的，先天的</td><td>an inborn talent<br>天生的才能</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She seems to have an inborn sense of rhythm.  她似乎天生就有节奏感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inbred</td><td>/ˈɪnˌbred/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>天生的，根深蒂固的</td><td>an inbred sense of duty<br>天生的责任感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He has an inbred distrust of large institutions.  他对大型机构有一种根深蒂固的不信任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ingrained</td><td>/ɪnˈɡreɪnd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>本质的，根深蒂固的</td><td>deeply ingrained habits<br>根深蒂固的习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Habits ingrained since childhood are extremely difficult to change in later life.  童年就已根深蒂固的习惯到了晚年极难改变。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 innate</td><td>/ɪˈneɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>天生的，固有的</td><td>an innate ability<br>天生的能力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Children have an innate curiosity about the world.  儿童对世界有一种天生的好奇心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 integral</td><td>/ˈɪntɪɡrəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可缺少的；完整的</td><td>an integral part<br>不可缺少的部分</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Careful revision is an integral part of writing any serious research paper.  仔细修改是撰写任何严肃研究论文不可缺少的部分。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intrinsic</td><td>/ɪnˈtrɪnsɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>固有的，内在的</td><td>intrinsic value<br>内在价值</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The painting has an intrinsic beauty independent of its price.  这幅画有着与价格无关的内在美。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 natural</td><td>/ˈnætʃərəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>自然的，正常的</td><td>a natural reaction<br>自然的反应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is natural to feel nervous before a big exam.  大考前紧张是很自然的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exotic</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɑːtɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>外来的，不同寻常的</td><td>exotic tropical fruit<br>外来的热带水果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The market sells exotic tropical fruit flown in each morning.  这个市场出售每天清晨空运来的外来热带水果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 extraneous</td><td>/ɪkˈstreɪniəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无关的，不重要的</td><td>extraneous information<br>无关的信息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please delete all extraneous details from the summary.  请把摘要中所有无关的细节删掉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 foreign</td><td>/ˈfɔːrən/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>外来的，异质的；外国的</td><td>a foreign object<br>异物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The surgeon removed a foreign object from the patient's eye.  外科医生从病人眼中取出一个异物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 adventitious</td><td>/ˌædvenˈtɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>外来的，后天的，非内在的</td><td>an adventitious circumstance<br>偶然的外部情况</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her success was not adventitious but the result of years of work.  她的成功不是偶然外来的，而是多年努力的结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 extrinsic</td><td>/eksˈtrɪnsɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>外在的，非固有的</td><td>extrinsic motivation<br>外在的动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Money is an extrinsic reward that rarely sustains real interest.  金钱是一种外在奖励，很少能维持真正的兴趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>indigent</b></td><td>/ˈɪndɪdʒənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>贫穷的，贫困的</td><td>indigent defendants<br>贫困的被告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clinic provides free care to indigent patients.  这家诊所为贫困病人提供免费医疗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 beggared</td><td>/ˈbeɡərd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>一无所有的，贫穷的</td><td>a beggared family<br>赤贫的家庭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The failed harvest left half the village beggared that winter.  歉收使那年冬天村里一半人家一无所有。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 destitute</td><td>/ˈdestəˌtuːt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>贫困的，穷困潦倒的</td><td>left destitute by war<br>因战争而穷困</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thousands of refugees arrived destitute and without documents.  数千名难民抵达时穷困潦倒且没有证件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impecunious</td><td>/ˌɪmpɪˈkjuːniəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贫穷的，没钱的</td><td>an impecunious student<br>身无分文的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 As an impecunious young painter, he lived on bread and tea.  作为一个穷困的年轻画家，他靠面包和茶为生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impoverished</td><td>/ɪmˈpɑːvərɪʃt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>贫困的；贫瘠的</td><td>an impoverished region<br>贫困地区</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 War left the once-prosperous city utterly impoverished for many years.  战争让这座曾繁荣的城市多年极度贫困。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 necessitous</del></td><td>/nəˈsɛsɪtəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贫困的；紧急的</td><td>necessitous families<br>贫困的家庭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The charity was founded to help necessitous widows and orphans.  该慈善机构成立时旨在帮助贫困的寡妇和孤儿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 needy</td><td>/ˈniːdi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>贫困的；情感上过度依赖的</td><td>needy families<br>贫困家庭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The charity distributes winter coats to needy children.  这家慈善机构向贫困儿童发放冬衣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 penniless</td><td>/ˈpeniləs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>身无分文的，贫穷的</td><td>a penniless student<br>身无分文的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He arrived in the city penniless and slept in the station.  他身无分文地来到这座城市，睡在车站里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 penurious</del></td><td>/pəˈnʊriəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贫困的；吝啬的</td><td>penurious circumstances<br>贫困的境况</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He spent a penurious youth in a crowded tenement.  他在拥挤的廉租房里度过了贫困的青年时期。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 threadbare</td><td>/ˈθredber/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>陈腐的；破旧的</td><td>a threadbare excuse<br>老掉牙的借口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He offered the same threadbare excuse for arriving late again.  他又用同一个老掉牙的借口解释迟到。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 affluent</td><td>/ˈæfluənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>富裕的，丰富的</td><td>an affluent suburb<br>富裕的郊区</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They moved to a more affluent neighborhood after the promotion.  升职后他们搬到了更富裕的街区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 opulent</td><td>/ˈɑːpjələnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>富裕的；华丽的</td><td>an opulent hotel lobby<br>豪华的酒店大厅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The opulent ballroom was lined with gold mirrors and velvet chairs.  这间华丽的舞厅摆满了金框镜子和天鹅绒椅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rich</td><td>/ˈrɪtʃ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>肥沃的，丰富的；富有的</td><td>rich farmland<br>肥沃的农田</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The delta has rich soil ideal for growing rice.  三角洲土壤肥沃，非常适合种稻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wealthy</td><td>/ˈwelθi/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>富有的，富裕的</td><td>a wealthy merchant<br>富有的商人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only the wealthy families could afford such an education.  只有富有的家庭才负担得起这样的教育。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>indispensable</b></td><td>/ˌɪndɪˈspensəbl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可缺少的，必需的</td><td>an indispensable tool<br>一件不可缺少的工具</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A good dictionary is indispensable to any serious translator.  一本好词典对任何认真的译者都不可缺少。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 critical</td><td>/ˈkrɪtɪkəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>关键的；批评的</td><td>a critical moment<br>关键时刻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Water supply is critical to the survival of this town.  供水对这座小镇的存续至关重要。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crucial</td><td>/ˈkruːʃl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>非常重要的，决定性的</td><td>a crucial decision<br>关键的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The next two weeks will be crucial to the project's survival.  接下来两周对这个项目的存亡至关重要。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imperative</td><td>/ɪmˈperətɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>命令的，强制性的；迫切的</td><td>an imperative need<br>迫切的需要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is imperative that we repair the dam before the rains arrive.  在雨季来临前修好大坝是迫切必需的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 necessary</td><td>/ˈnesəˌseri/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>必需的，必要的；必需品</td><td>a necessary expense<br>一项必要的开支</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Clean water is necessary for even the simplest household.  即便最简朴的家庭也需要干净的水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 necessitous</del></td><td>/nəˈsɛsɪtəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贫困的；紧急的</td><td>necessitous families<br>贫困的家庭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The charity was founded to help necessitous widows and orphans.  该慈善机构成立时旨在帮助贫困的寡妇和孤儿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 needed</td><td>/ˈniːdəd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>必需的，需要的</td><td>much needed support<br>急需的支持</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Aid workers finally delivered the needed supplies to the remote village.  救援人员终于把急需的物资送到偏远村庄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 required</td><td>/rɪˈkwaɪərd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>必需的，规定的</td><td>required reading<br>必读材料</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A signed form is required before the trip can begin.  出行前必须提交一份签好的表格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 requisite</td><td>/ˈrekwɪzɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>必需品；必要条件</td><td>requisite skills for the job<br>工作所需的技能</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Patience is a requisite for anyone hoping to teach very young children.  耐心是任何想教幼儿的人必备的条件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vital</td><td>/ˈvaɪtəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满活力的；极重要的</td><td>full of vital energy<br>充满生命活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Regular exercise keeps older people vital and mentally alert.  经常锻炼使老年人保持活力和头脑清醒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dispensable</td><td>/dɪˈspensəbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可省去的，非必需的</td><td>a dispensable luxury<br>可有可无的奢侈品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In a crisis, such formal ceremonies are entirely dispensable.  危机之中，这类正式仪式完全可以省去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>indoctrinate</b></td><td>/ɪnˈdɑːktrɪneɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>教育，灌输思想</td><td>indoctrinate new recruits<br>向新兵灌输思想</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The academy tried to indoctrinate new recruits with rigid political ideas.  该学院试图向新兵灌输僵化的政治思想。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 educate</td><td>/ˈedʒəˌkeɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>教育，培养</td><td>educate the public<br>教育公众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The campaign educated farmers about safer ways to store grain.  这项活动教育农民如何更安全地储存粮食。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 instruct</td><td>/ɪnˈstrʌkt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>教导，指导；指示</td><td>instruct new employees<br>指导新员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She instructed the class in basic first aid last week.  上周她教这个班基本的急救知识。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lesson</td><td>/ˈlesən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>课；教训</td><td>learn a hard lesson<br>学到惨痛教训</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The failure taught him a lesson he would never forget.  这次失败给了他一个永生难忘的教训。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 teach</td><td>/ˈtiːtʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>教，讲授</td><td>teach students to think<br>教学生思考</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has taught chemistry at the local high school for years.  她在当地高中教化学已有多年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 train</td><td>/ˈtreɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>训练，培养</td><td>train new employees<br>培训新员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach trains young swimmers every morning before dawn.  教练每天黎明前训练年轻游泳选手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tutor</td><td>/ˈtuːtər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>辅导；导师，家教</td><td>tutor students in math<br>辅导学生数学</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He tutors two neighborhood children in reading after school.  他放学后辅导两个邻家孩子阅读。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 learn</td><td>/ˈlɜːrn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>学习；得知</td><td>learn a new language<br>学一门新语言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Children learn languages far more quickly than most adults do.  儿童学语言比大多数成年人快得多。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 study</td><td>/ˈstʌdi/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>学习，研究</td><td>study hard for exams<br>为考试用功</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She studies medieval history at a university in Scotland.  她在苏格兰一所大学研究中世纪史。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>indolent</b></td><td>/ˈɪndələnt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>懒惰的</td><td>an indolent young man<br>懒散的年轻人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Too indolent to look for work, he simply lay on the couch all summer.  他懒得去找工作，整个夏天就躺在沙发上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lazy</td><td>/ˈleɪzi/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>懒惰的</td><td>too lazy to cook<br>懒得做饭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was too lazy to walk the dog on cold mornings.  寒冷的早晨他懒得去遛狗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 idle</td><td>/ˈaɪdl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>闲置的，未使用的；懒散的</td><td>idle machines<br>闲置的机器</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Half the factory's machines stood idle for most of last year.  这家工厂一半的机器去年大部分时间都闲置着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shiftless</td><td>/ˈʃɪftləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>胸无大志的，懒惰的</td><td>a shiftless drifter<br>游手好闲的流浪者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His father dismissed him as a shiftless young man without ambition.  他父亲斥他为没有志向、游手好闲的年轻人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slothful</td><td>/ˈsloʊθfəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>懒惰的，迟缓的</td><td>slothful habits<br>懒散的习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of easy living had made the once-diligent clerk slothful.  多年安逸生活使这位曾经勤勉的职员变得懒散。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sluggish</td><td>/ˈslʌɡɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>行动迟缓的，懒散的</td><td>feel sluggish all morning<br>整个上午都懒洋洋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He felt sluggish all morning and could barely finish his simplest chores.  他整个上午都懒洋洋的，连最简单的杂事都几乎做不完。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 diligent</td><td>/ˈdɪlɪdʒənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>勤勉的，辛勤的</td><td>a diligent student<br>勤奋的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A diligent editor checked every footnote before the manuscript went to the printer.  一位勤勉的编辑在书稿付印前核对了每一条脚注。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 industrious</td><td>/ɪnˈdʌstriəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>勤勉的，刻苦的</td><td>an industrious worker<br>勤劳的工人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The most industrious student in the class reviewed her notes every single night.  班里最勤奋的学生每晚都复习笔记。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>induce</b></td><td>/ɪnˈduːs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>引发，导致</td><td>induce a sense of calm<br>引发平静感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The soft music and dim lighting induced a deep sense of calm in everyone.  轻柔的音乐和昏暗的灯光让每个人都产生了深深的平静感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 engender</td><td>/ɪnˈdʒendər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>引起，使发展</td><td>engender trust<br>产生信任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Small acts of honesty engender trust more surely than grand promises do.  小小的诚实之举比宏大的承诺更能可靠地产生信任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 beget</td><td>/bɪˈɡet/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>引起，产生；生育</td><td>violence begets violence<br>暴力引发暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Careless words often beget quarrels that last for years.  轻率的言语常引发持续多年的争吵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bring about</td><td>/brɪŋ əˈbaʊt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>导致，引起</td><td>bring about real change<br>带来真正的改变</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New technology brought about profound changes in the publishing industry.  新技术给出版业带来了深刻变化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cause</td><td>/ˈkɔːz/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>原因；导致，引起</td><td>the root cause<br>根本原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy rain caused the small stream to flood the road.  大雨使这条小溪淹了公路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 generate</td><td>/ˈdʒenəreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>产生，引起；发电</td><td>generate interest<br>引起兴趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The trailer generated enormous interest weeks before the release.  预告片在上映数周前就引起了极大兴趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 produce</td><td>/prəˈduːs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>生产，制造；产品</td><td>produce steel locally<br>本地生产钢材</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The factory can produce ten thousand units of steel every month.  这家工厂每月能生产一万单位钢材。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 yield</td><td>/ˈjiːld/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>产生，出产；屈服，让出</td><td>yield results<br>产生结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Months of patient testing finally yielded a usable vaccine.  数月耐心的试验终于产出可用的疫苗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>indulgent</b></td><td>/ɪnˈdʌldʒənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（对己）放纵的，（对他人）纵容的</td><td>an indulgent parent<br>溺爱孩子的家长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her indulgent grandparents let her eat whatever she wanted.  溺爱她的祖父母让她想吃什么就吃什么。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decadent</td><td>/ˈdekədənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>堕落的，腐败的</td><td>a decadent ruling class<br>腐败堕落的统治阶层</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He described the court as decadent, idle, and blind to reform.  他形容那个宫廷腐败堕落、无所作为、对改革视而不见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 forbearing</td><td>/fɔrˈbɛrɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽容的，有耐心的</td><td>a forbearing teacher<br>宽容的老师</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His forbearing manner calmed even the rudest customers.  他宽容的态度连最无礼的顾客都能安抚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lenient</td><td>/ˈliːniənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽大仁慈的</td><td>a lenient sentence<br>从轻的判决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge handed down a lenient sentence because of the boy's age.  由于这个男孩年纪小，法官作出了从轻的判决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 luxurious</td><td>/lʌɡˈʒʊriəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>奢侈的</td><td>a luxurious hotel suite<br>豪华的酒店套房</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They spent their anniversary in a luxurious hotel suite overlooking the harbor.  他们在一间俯瞰港口的豪华酒店套房里度过了结婚纪念日。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tolerant</td><td>/ˈtɑːlərənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽容的，容忍的</td><td>tolerant of criticism<br>能容忍批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A good editor is tolerant of odd but honest writing.  好编辑对古怪却真诚的文字是宽容的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 sybaritic</del></td><td>/ˌsɪbəˈrɪtɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>穷奢极欲的，享乐的</td><td>a sybaritic lifestyle<br>穷奢极欲的生活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The emperor's sybaritic banquets lasted from dusk until dawn.  皇帝穷奢极欲的宴会从黄昏持续到黎明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ascetic</td><td>/əˈsetɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>苦行的，禁欲的；苦行者</td><td>an ascetic lifestyle<br>苦修的生活方式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monk led an ascetic life on bread and cold water.  这位僧人以面包和冷水过着自我克制的生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 5

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>indurate</b></del></td><td>/ˈɪndjʊreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使变硬；使冷酷无情</td><td>indurate the heart<br>使心变得冷硬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of betrayal had indurated his heart against every plea for sympathy.  多年的背叛使他的心变得冷硬，对任何求情都无动于衷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 affectless</del></td><td>/ˈæfektləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无情感的，冷漠的</td><td>an affectless voice<br>毫无感情的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness described the crime in a flat, affectless tone.  证人用平淡无情感的语调描述这桩罪行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 callous</td><td>/ˈkæləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无同情心的，冷漠的</td><td>a callous remark<br>冷漠无情的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His callous joke about the accident shocked everyone in the room.  他拿这场事故开的冷酷玩笑震惊了房间里的每个人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 heartless</td><td>/ˈhɑːrtləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无情的，狠心的</td><td>a heartless decision<br>无情的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was heartless to fire her the week before Christmas.  在圣诞节前一周解雇她真是无情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inhumane</td><td>/ˌɪnhjuˈmeɪn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不人道的，残忍的</td><td>inhumane treatment of prisoners<br>对囚犯的残忍对待</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics condemned the inhumane conditions inside the overcrowded shelter.  批评者谴责了过度拥挤的收容所内不人道的条件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 merciless</td><td>/ˈmɜːrsələs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>毫无仁慈的，无情的</td><td>a merciless critic<br>毫不留情的批评者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The merciless summer sun beat down on the empty streets.  无情的夏日阳光炙烤着空荡的街道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obdurate</td><td>/ˈɑːbdərət/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的；冷酷无情的</td><td>remain obdurate<br>仍然固执不改</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The landlord was obdurate and refused to lower the rent.  房东顽固不化，拒绝降低房租。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pitiless</td><td>/ˈpɪtiləs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>没有同情心的</td><td>a pitiless judge<br>冷酷无情的法官</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pitiless judge showed no mercy to the young first offender.  那位冷酷无情的法官对年轻的初犯毫不留情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ruthless</td><td>/ˈruːθləs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>没有怜悯心的，残忍的</td><td>a ruthless dictator<br>残忍无情的独裁者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The general was ruthless with anyone who questioned his orders.  将军对任何质疑其命令的人都残忍无情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unsparing</td><td>/ʌnˈsperɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>毫不留情的；毫不吝惜的</td><td>unsparing criticism<br>毫不留情的批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The review was unsparing about the actor's weak performance.  这篇评论对该演员糟糕的表演毫不留情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cold-blooded</td><td>/ˌkoʊldˈblʌdɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷血的，无感情的</td><td>a cold-blooded killer<br>冷血的杀手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court described the crime as cold-blooded and carefully planned.  法庭形容这起罪行冷血且经过精心策划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 concrete</td><td>/ˈkɑːŋkriːt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>具体的，实在的；明确的</td><td>concrete evidence<br>确凿的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The detective needed concrete evidence rather than clever theories.  侦探需要确凿的证据，而不是巧妙的推论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 congeal</td><td>/kənˈdʒiːl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>凝固，固化</td><td>congeal into a mass<br>凝结成块</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The gravy congealed as soon as the plate cooled.  盘子一凉，肉汁就凝住了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 firm</td><td>/ˈfɜːrm/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>坚定的，坚固的</td><td>a firm decision<br>坚定的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She remained firm despite enormous pressure from the board.  尽管董事会施加巨大压力，她依然坚定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 freeze</td><td>/ˈfriːz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>结冰，冻住；冻结</td><td>freeze solid<br>冻得结实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pond will freeze if the temperature drops below zero tonight.  如果今晚气温降到零度以下，池塘会结冰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 set</td><td>/ˈset/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词、形容词</td><td>使朝向，安置；设定</td><td>set a new course<br>设定新航向</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The captain set the ship's course toward the northern islands.  船长把船的航向设定为朝北方群岛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 solidify</td><td>/səˈlɪdəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使凝固；使稳固</td><td>solidify into a mass<br>凝固成一块</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The melted wax solidified quickly in the cold room.  融化的蜡在冷房间里很快凝固了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 charitable</td><td>/ˈtʃærətəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽容的，仁慈的</td><td>a charitable view<br>宽容的看法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He took a charitable view of his student's careless mistake.  他对学生的粗心错误持宽容态度。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 compassionate</td><td>/kəmˈpæʃənət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有同情心的，慈悲的</td><td>compassionate care<br>充满关怀的照护</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A compassionate nurse sat with the frightened child all night.  一位有同情心的护士整夜陪着那个受惊的孩子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 humane</td><td>/hjuˈmeɪn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>人道的，仁慈的</td><td>humane treatment of prisoners<br>人道地对待囚犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Activists demanded more humane conditions inside the detention center.  活动人士要求改善拘留中心内的人道条件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 kindhearted</td><td>/ˌkaɪndˈhɑrtɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>好心的，仁慈的</td><td>a kindhearted neighbor<br>好心的邻居</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A kindhearted stranger paid for the child's bus ticket.  一位好心的陌生人替那孩子付了车票。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 merciful</td><td>/ˈmɜːrsɪfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>仁慈的，宽厚的</td><td>a merciful judge<br>宽仁的法官</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court was merciful and gave him only a small fine.  法庭很宽仁，只罚了他少许款项。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tender</td><td>/ˈtendər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>温柔的，心软同情的；正式提出</td><td>a tender heart<br>一颗温柔同情的心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has a tender heart and cannot bear to see animals suffer.  她心地温软，见不得动物受苦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 liquefy</td><td>/ˈlɪkwəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>使液化，变成液体</td><td>liquefy the mixture<br>使混合物液化</td></tr>
