<tr><td colspan="7">例 The snake looks frightening but is completely harmless to humans.  这条蛇看起来可怕，但对人完全无害。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inoffensive</td><td>/ˌɪnəˈfensɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无害的，不讨人厌的</td><td>inoffensive background music<br>不讨人厌的背景音乐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film is bland but inoffensive enough for family viewing.  这部电影平淡，但足够温和，适合全家观看。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nontoxic</td><td>/nɑːnˈtɑːksɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无毒的</td><td>nontoxic cleaning products<br>无毒清洁用品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The paint is nontoxic and safe for children's rooms.  这种涂料无毒，适用于儿童房。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 safe</td><td>/ˈseɪf/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>安全的，可靠的</td><td>a safe choice<br>稳妥的选择</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Taking the train is the safe choice in this weather.  这种天气坐火车是稳妥的选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bland</td><td>/blænd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无趣的</td><td>a bland performance<br>平淡的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The critics called it a bland performance with no real feeling.  评论家称这是一场毫无真情、十分无趣的表演。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insipid</td><td>/ɪnˈsɪpɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>平淡的，无聊的</td><td>an insipid conversation<br>无聊的谈话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film was so insipid that half the audience fell asleep.  这部电影平淡无聊，半数观众都睡着了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 neutral</td><td>/ˈnuːtrəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>中立的；无刺激性的</td><td>remain neutral in disputes<br>在争端中保持中立</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Switzerland stayed neutral throughout most of the twentieth century.  瑞士在二十世纪大部分时间保持中立。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 sapless</del></td><td>/ˈsæpləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无活力的，枯干的</td><td>a sapless performance<br>毫无生气的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old comedian gave a sapless performance that bored everyone.  这位老喜剧演员的表演毫无生气，让所有人都很无聊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 damaging</td><td>/ˈdæmɪdʒɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的，破坏性的</td><td>damaging to reputation<br>有损声誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The leaked memo proved damaging to the minister's career.  泄露的备忘录对部长的仕途造成损害。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 detrimental</td><td>/ˌdetrəˈmentəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的，不利的</td><td>detrimental to your health<br>对你的健康有害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Too much screen time is detrimental to a child's eyesight.  过多看屏幕对孩子的视力有害。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 harmful</td><td>/ˈhɑːrmfəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的，造成伤害的</td><td>harmful to health<br>对健康有害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Too much screen time can be harmful to a child's sleep.  屏幕时间过长可能有害于孩子的睡眠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 injurious</td><td>/ɪnˈdʒʊriəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的，造成伤害的</td><td>injurious to health<br>对健康有害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Long hours of night work can be injurious to your health.  长时间夜班工作可能有害健康。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 noxious</td><td>/ˈnɑːkʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的，有毒的；产生强烈厌恶的</td><td>noxious fumes<br>有毒的烟气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Noxious fumes from the factory forced the school to close for two days.  工厂排出的有毒烟气迫使学校停课两天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pernicious</td><td>/pərˈnɪʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的</td><td>a pernicious rumor<br>极有害的谣言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A pernicious rumor about the school spread quickly through the town.  一则关于学校的极有害谣言迅速在镇上传开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 invasive</td><td>/ɪnˈveɪsɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>侵入的；侵略性的</td><td>an invasive species<br>入侵物种</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Gardeners struggle to remove the invasive vine from fences.  园丁们努力清除篱笆上的入侵藤蔓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 provoking</td><td>/prəˈvoʊkɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>恼人的，令人恼火的</td><td>a provoking question<br>招人恼火的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His provoking silence made the argument even worse.  他那令人恼火的沉默使争论更糟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>innovative</b></td><td>/ˈɪnəveɪtɪv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>创新性的</td><td>innovative design<br>创新的设计</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The firm survived the crisis thanks to an innovative approach to distribution.  这家公司靠创新的分销方式度过了危机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 creative</td><td>/kriˈeɪtɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有创造力的，创新的</td><td>a creative solution<br>有创意的办法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She found a creative solution to the seating problem.  她为座位问题想出了有创意的办法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ingenious</td><td>/ɪnˈdʒiːniəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>聪明的，有创造才能的</td><td>an ingenious solution<br>巧妙的解决办法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She devised an ingenious method for storing rainwater during the dry season.  她想出了一个在旱季储存雨水的巧妙方法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inventive</td><td>/ɪnˈventɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有创造力的，善发明的</td><td>an inventive chef<br>有创意的厨师</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Engineers found an inventive way to recycle factory waste heat.  工程师找到了回收工厂余热的创新办法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 original</td><td>/əˈrɪdʒənl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>创新的；最初的，最早的</td><td>an original idea<br>有创意的想法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her proposal was genuinely original, unlike the tired ideas we usually hear.  她的提案确实新颖，不像我们常听到的那些陈旧想法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conservative</td><td>/kənˈsɜːrvətɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>守旧的，不愿改变的；不招摇的，低调的</td><td>a conservative estimate<br>保守的估计</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her parents were too conservative to accept such an unconventional career choice.  她的父母太守旧，无法接受这样不合常规的职业选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hidebound</td><td>/ˈhaɪdbaʊnd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>死板的，极度保守的</td><td>hidebound traditions<br>僵化的传统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hidebound innkeeper flatly refused to install internet for guests.  这位死板的旅店老板断然拒绝为客人装网络。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>inquisitive</b></td><td>/ɪnˈkwɪzətɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>过分好奇的</td><td>an inquisitive child<br>好奇多问的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our inquisitive neighbor asks about every visitor who parks on our street.  我们那位过分好奇的邻居会打听每个在我们街上停车的来客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 curious</td><td>/ˈkjʊriəs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>好奇的；奇怪的</td><td>curious about everything<br>对一切都好奇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children were curious about the strange machine in the yard.  孩子们对院子里那台奇怪的机器很好奇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inquiring</td><td>/ɪnˈkwaɪərɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>好问的，爱探究的</td><td>an inquiring mind<br>好探究的头脑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Science rewards an inquiring mind more than a retentive one.  科学更青睐好探究的头脑而非记性好的头脑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 investigative</td><td>/ɪnˈvestɪɡeɪtɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>调查的，探究的</td><td>investigative journalism<br>调查性新闻报道</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her investigative reporting exposed years of financial fraud.  她的调查报道揭露了多年的财务欺诈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prying</td><td>/ˈpraɪɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>爱打探的，窥探的</td><td>prying neighbors<br>爱打探的邻居</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She drew the curtains to escape prying eyes.  她拉上窗帘以避开窥探的目光。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 indifferent</td><td>/ɪnˈdɪfrənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不感兴趣的，冷漠的；平庸的</td><td>indifferent to criticism<br>对批评漠不关心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is entirely indifferent to what her critics say.  她对批评者说什么完全无所谓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unconcerned</td><td>/ˌʌnkənˈsɜːrnd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不关心的，漠不关心的</td><td>unconcerned about risk<br>对风险漠不关心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She seemed entirely unconcerned about the noise from the street.  她似乎完全不在意街上的噪音。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 uninterested</td><td>/ʌnˈɪntrəstɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不感兴趣的，漠不关心的</td><td>uninterested in politics<br>对政治不感兴趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The students seemed uninterested in the long lecture.  学生们对这场长讲座似乎不感兴趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>insensible</b></td><td>/ɪnˈsensəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无知觉的，昏迷的</td><td>insensible to pain<br>感觉不到疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The blow left him insensible for several minutes.  这一击使他几分钟内失去了知觉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 anesthetic</td><td>/ˌænəsˈθetɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>麻醉剂；麻醉的</td><td>under general anesthetic<br>在全身麻醉下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dentist gave her a local anesthetic before drilling the tooth.  牙医在钻牙之前给她做了局部麻醉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insensate</td><td>/ɪnˈsenseɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无感觉的；无理性的</td><td>insensate to pain<br>对疼痛毫无感觉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He lay insensate on the floor for several minutes.  他失去知觉地在地板上躺了好几分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 senseless</td><td>/ˈsensləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无意义的，愚蠢的</td><td>senseless violence<br>毫无意义的暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cutting the safety budget now would be completely senseless.  现在削减安全预算完全是愚蠢之举。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unfeeling</td><td>/ənˈfiːlɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无感情的，冷酷的</td><td>an unfeeling response<br>冷漠的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It seemed unfeeling to laugh at such a moment.  在这样的时刻发笑显得毫无感情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unconscious</td><td>/ʌnˈkɑːnʃəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无意识的；昏迷的</td><td>an unconscious habit<br>无意识的习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The driver lay unconscious on the road until help arrived.  司机躺在路上昏迷不醒，直到救援到来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conscious</td><td>/ˈkɑːnʃəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有意识的，察觉到的</td><td>a conscious decision<br>有意识的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He made a conscious effort to speak more slowly on stage.  他有意识地努力在台上讲得慢一些。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>inconsistent</b></td><td>/ˌɪnkənˈsɪstənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不一致的，矛盾的</td><td>inconsistent with the facts<br>与事实不符</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His testimony was inconsistent with the facts established by the police.  他的证词与警方查明的事实不符。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conflicting</td><td>/kənˈflɪktɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冲突的，矛盾的</td><td>conflicting accounts<br>相互矛盾的说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Witnesses gave conflicting accounts of the driver's speed.  证人对司机车速的说法相互矛盾。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disagreeing</td><td>/dɪsəˈɡriːɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不一致的，有分歧的</td><td>disagreeing accounts<br>相互矛盾的说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witnesses gave disagreeing accounts of the accident.  证人对事故给出了互相矛盾的说法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 discordant</td><td>/dɪˈskɔːrdənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不一致的，不和谐的</td><td>discordant opinions<br>不一致的意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Discordant voices from the back ruined the quiet ceremony.  后面不和谐的声音破坏了安静的仪式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incompatible</td><td>/ˌɪnkəmˈpætəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不相容的，矛盾的</td><td>incompatible goals<br>互不相容的目标</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rapid growth and strict conservation seemed incompatible goals here.  在这里快速发展与严格保护似乎是矛盾的目标。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incongruous</td><td>/ɪnˈkɑːŋɡruəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不协调的，不相称的</td><td>an incongruous mixture<br>不协调的混合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His loud tie looked incongruous with the somber gray suit.  他花哨的领带与深灰西装很不协调。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 inconsonant</del></td><td>/ɪnˈkɑːnsənənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不一致的，不协调的</td><td>inconsonant with the facts<br>与事实不符</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His account is inconsonant with what the witnesses reported.  他的说法与证人所述不一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 inharmonious</del></td><td>/ˌɪnhɑːrˈmoʊniəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不和谐的，不协调的</td><td>inharmonious colors<br>不协调的颜色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new wing looked inharmonious beside the old stone chapel.  新翼楼在古老石砌小教堂旁显得不协调。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 accordant</del></td><td>/əˈkɔrdənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>一致的，符合的</td><td>accordant with the law<br>与法律相符</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ruling was accordant with decisions made in earlier cases.  该裁决与早先案件的判决一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 agreeing</td><td>/əˈɡriːɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>一致的，相符的</td><td>agreeing testimony<br>一致的证词</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two agreeing witnesses strengthened the prosecution's case at the appeal hearing.  两位说法一致的证人在上诉听证会上加强了检方的论证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 compatible</td><td>/kəmˈpætəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>一致的，能共存的</td><td>compatible with the system<br>与该系统兼容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new driver is compatible with both older printers.  新驱动与两款旧打印机都兼容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 congruent</td><td>/ˈkɑːŋɡruənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>和谐一致的；全等的</td><td>congruent with the facts<br>与事实相符</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His account is congruent with the facts we already know.  他的说法与我们已知的事实相符。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 consistent</td><td>/kənˈsɪstənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>一致的；始终如一的</td><td>consistent with the evidence<br>与证据相符</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His account is consistent with what the neighbors reported.  他的说法与邻居们所述一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 harmonious</td><td>/hɑːrˈmoʊniəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>和谐的，融洽的；悦耳的</td><td>a harmonious relationship<br>和谐的关系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two neighbors maintained a harmonious relationship for over thirty years.  这两位邻居三十多年来保持着融洽的关系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>insentient</b></del></td><td>/ɪnˈsentiənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无感觉的，无知觉的</td><td>insentient matter<br>无知觉的物质</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ancient philosophers debated whether plants are insentient or capable of feeling pain.  古代哲学家争论植物是无知觉的还是能感到痛苦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impassive</td><td>/ɪmˈpæsɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷漠的，无感情的</td><td>remain impassive under pressure<br>压力下仍无动于衷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His face stayed impassive as the verdict was read aloud.  宣读判决时，他的脸上始终毫无表情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insensate</td><td>/ɪnˈsenseɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无感觉的；无理性的</td><td>insensate to pain<br>对疼痛毫无感觉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He lay insensate on the floor for several minutes.  他失去知觉地在地板上躺了好几分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insensible</td><td>/ɪnˈsensəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无知觉的，昏迷的</td><td>insensible to pain<br>感觉不到疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The blow left him insensible for several minutes.  这一击使他几分钟内失去了知觉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 senseless</td><td>/ˈsensləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无意义的，愚蠢的</td><td>senseless violence<br>毫无意义的暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cutting the safety budget now would be completely senseless.  现在削减安全预算完全是愚蠢之举。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unresponsive</td><td>/ˌʌnrɪˈspɑːnsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷淡的，无反应的</td><td>an unresponsive audience<br>毫无反应的观众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The comedian struggled before a cold, unresponsive audience.  这位喜剧演员面对冷淡无反应的观众很吃力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 impercipient</del></td><td>/ˌɪmpərˈsɪpiənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无感知力的</td><td>impercipient of danger<br>察觉不到危险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crowd seemed impercipient of the rising tension around them.  人群似乎察觉不到周围日益紧张的气氛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unwise</td><td>/ʌnˈwaɪz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不明智的，愚蠢的</td><td>an unwise decision<br>不明智的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Borrowing more money now would be deeply unwise.  现在再借钱是很不明智的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 perceiving</td><td>/pərˈsiːvɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有感知力的，能察觉的</td><td>a perceiving mind<br>善于察觉的头脑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only a perceiving reader notices the author's hidden joke.  只有敏于察觉的读者才会注意到作者隐藏的玩笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sensible</td><td>/ˈsensəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明智的，合理的</td><td>a sensible choice<br>明智的选择</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wearing boots was a sensible choice in that mud.  在那种泥地里穿靴子是明智的选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sensitive</td><td>/ˈsensətɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>敏感的，灵敏的</td><td>sensitive to light<br>对光敏感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This instrument is sensitive enough to detect the faintest vibration.  这台仪器灵敏到能测出最微弱的震动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 discerning</td><td>/dɪˈsɜːrnɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有洞察力的，眼光敏锐的</td><td>a discerning listener<br>有鉴赏力的听众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The wine appeals to discerning drinkers rather than casual buyers.  这款酒吸引有鉴赏力的饮者而非随意的买家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 insightful</td><td>/ˈɪnˌsaɪtfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有洞察力的，有见识的</td><td>an insightful comment<br>有洞察力的评论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her insightful reading of the poem impressed the whole class.  她对这首诗富有洞见的解读让全班佩服。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>inconclusive</b></td><td>/ˌɪnkənˈkluːsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>没有定论的</td><td>inconclusive evidence<br>无定论的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tests were inconclusive, so they ran them again.  检测没有定论，于是他们又做了一次。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indecisive</td><td>/ˌɪndɪˈsaɪsɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>犹豫不决的；非决定性的</td><td>hopelessly indecisive<br>极其优柔寡断</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is too indecisive to lead the team.  他太犹豫不决，无法带领团队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unclear</td><td>/ʌnˈklɪr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不清楚的，不明确的</td><td>remain unclear<br>仍不明确</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is still unclear who made the final decision.  谁做的最终决定仍不清楚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 absolute</td><td>/ˈæbsəluːt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>专制的；无限的；纯净不掺杂的；确凿的</td><td>absolute power<br>绝对的权力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The king held absolute power over his subjects.  国王对臣民拥有绝对权力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 clear</td><td>/ˈklɪr/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明确的，确凿的；清楚的</td><td>a clear majority<br>明显的多数</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party won by a clear majority of votes.  该党以明显多数票获胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conclusive</td><td>/kənˈkluːsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>决定性的，无可置疑的</td><td>conclusive proof<br>决定性的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tests were not conclusive, so the doctors ordered more.  检测结果并不确定，医生便要求再做检查。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 decisive</td><td>/dɪˈsaɪsɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>决定性的；果断的</td><td>a decisive victory<br>决定性的胜利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her testimony was decisive in the trial.  她的证词在审判中起了决定性作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 definitive</td><td>/dɪˈfɪnɪtɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>确定的，不容置疑的；权威的</td><td>a definitive answer<br>确定无疑的答案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We still have no definitive answer from the committee.  我们仍未从委员会得到确定的答复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>insipid</b></td><td>/ɪnˈsɪpɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>平淡的，无聊的</td><td>an insipid conversation<br>无聊的谈话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film was so insipid that half the audience fell asleep.  这部电影平淡无聊，半数观众都睡着了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 banal</td><td>/bəˈnɑːl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非原创的，陈腐的</td><td>banal remarks<br>陈腐的言论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film's ending was so banal that the audience laughed.  这部电影的结尾陈腐得让观众笑了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bland</td><td>/blænd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无趣的</td><td>a bland performance<br>平淡的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The critics called it a bland performance with no real feeling.  评论家称这是一场毫无真情、十分无趣的表演。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 driveling</del></td><td>/ˈdrɪvəlɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>说傻话的，胡扯的</td><td>driveling nonsense<br>愚蠢的胡话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He dismissed the report as driveling nonsense from amateurs.  他把这份报告斥为业余者的愚蠢胡话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prosaic</td><td>/prəˈzeɪɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>单调的，常见的</td><td>prosaic details<br>平淡的细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Behind the legend lies a prosaic story about a lost receipt.  传说背后是一个关于遗失收据的平淡故事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tedious</td><td>/ˈtiːdiəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冗长乏味的</td><td>a tedious explanation<br>冗长乏味的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager gave a tedious explanation of the new filing system.  经理对新的归档系统作了冗长乏味的解释。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uninteresting</td><td>/ʌnˈɪntrəstɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无趣的，乏味的</td><td>an uninteresting report<br>乏味的报告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The food at the small hotel was cheap but completely uninteresting.  这家小旅馆的食物便宜，却毫无趣味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vapid</td><td>/ˈvæpɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无趣的，乏味的</td><td>a vapid conversation<br>乏味的谈话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party ended in vapid conversation about traffic and the weather.  聚会最后只剩下关于交通和天气的乏味闲谈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enchanting</td><td>/ɪnˈtʃæntɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>迷人的，醉人的</td><td>an enchanting melody<br>醉人的旋律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The garden looked enchanting under the soft light of lanterns.  花园在灯笼柔和的光线下显得十分迷人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>insolent</b></td><td>/ˈɪnsələnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗野的，无礼的</td><td>an insolent tone<br>无礼的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The student's insolent reply to the teacher shocked the entire classroom.  这名学生对老师无礼的回答震惊了整个教室。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arrogant</td><td>/ˈærəɡənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>傲慢的，自大的</td><td>an arrogant manner<br>傲慢的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His arrogant remarks cost him the respect of his colleagues.  他傲慢的言论使他失去了同事的尊重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 audacious</td><td>/ɔːˈdeɪʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>大胆的，（但往往）愚勇的；令人吃惊的</td><td>an audacious plan<br>大胆的计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The general approved an audacious plan to cross the river at night.  将军批准了一个夜间渡河的大胆计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bold</td><td>/ˈboʊld/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>大胆的，勇敢的；醒目的</td><td>a bold move<br>大胆的举动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was a bold decision to leave a secure job.  辞去稳定的工作是个大胆的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 haughty</td><td>/ˈhɔːti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>傲慢的，目中无人的</td><td>a haughty manner<br>傲慢的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave the waiter a haughty look and said nothing.  她傲慢地看了服务员一眼，什么也没说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 supercilious</td><td>/ˌsuːpərˈsɪliəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>高傲的，傲慢的</td><td>a supercilious smile<br>傲慢的微笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A supercilious smile crossed his face when he heard my question.  听到我的问题，他脸上掠过傲慢的微笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impertinent</td><td>/ɪmˈpɜːrtnənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不相关的；无礼的，鲁莽的</td><td>an impertinent remark<br>无礼的言论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer objected that the question was impertinent to the charges.  律师反对说，这个问题与指控无关。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impudent</td><td>/ˈɪmpjədənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>放肆大胆的，无礼的</td><td>an impudent remark<br>一句无礼的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The impudent student argued with the professor in class.  这个无礼的学生在课上与教授争辩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 courteous</td><td>/ˈkɜːrtiəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>礼貌的，谦恭有礼的</td><td>a courteous reply<br>礼貌的答复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even when customers shouted, the young clerk remained calm and courteous.  即使顾客大声嚷嚷，那位年轻店员仍然平静而有礼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 polite</td><td>/pəˈlaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有礼貌的，客气的</td><td>make polite conversation<br>客套地寒暄</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is polite to greet your neighbors in the morning.  早上向邻居问好是有礼貌的做法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 9

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>insouciant</b></td><td>/ɪnˈsuːsiənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无忧虑的，不在乎的</td><td>an insouciant shrug<br>满不在乎地耸肩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He answered the harsh criticism with an insouciant shrug and a smile.  他对严厉的批评只是满不在乎地耸耸肩，笑了笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 carefree</td><td>/ˈkerfriː/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无忧无虑的</td><td>a carefree summer<br>无忧无虑的夏天</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We spent a carefree summer swimming in the lake every day.  我们度过了一个无忧无虑的夏天，每天在湖里游泳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 casual</td><td>/ˈkæʒəwəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>随意的，非故意的</td><td>a casual remark<br>随口的一句话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A casual remark at dinner accidentally revealed the whole secret.  晚餐时随口一句话意外揭出了整个秘密。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indifferent</td><td>/ɪnˈdɪfrənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不感兴趣的，冷漠的；平庸的</td><td>indifferent to criticism<br>对批评漠不关心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is entirely indifferent to what her critics say.  她对批评者说什么完全无所谓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nonchalant</td><td>/ˌnɑːnʃəˈlɑːnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>漫不经心的，淡然的</td><td>a nonchalant shrug<br>漫不经心地一耸肩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He answered the accusation with a nonchalant shrug and changed the subject.  他冷淡地一耸肩回应指控，然后换了话题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unconcerned</td><td>/ˌʌnkənˈsɜːrnd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不关心的，漠不关心的</td><td>unconcerned about risk<br>对风险漠不关心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She seemed entirely unconcerned about the noise from the street.  她似乎完全不在意街上的噪音。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 anxious</td><td>/ˈæŋkʃəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>焦虑的，担心的</td><td>anxious about the results<br>为结果焦虑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She grew anxious as the deadline drew closer.  截止日临近，她越来越焦虑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 careworn</td><td>/ˈkerwɔːrn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>憔悴的，饱受忧虑的</td><td>a careworn face<br>憔悴的面容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her careworn face brightened when the children ran into the room.  孩子们跑进房间时，她憔悴的脸上亮了起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>instate</b></td><td>/ɪnˈsteɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>任命</td><td>instate a new director<br>任命新主管</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board voted to instate a new director before the quarter ended.  董事会投票决定在本季度结束前任命一位新主管。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 appoint</td><td>/əˈpɔɪnt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>任命，指定</td><td>appoint a committee chair<br>任命委员会主席</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board appointed her chief executive last spring.  董事会去年春天任命她为首席执行官。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 designate</td><td>/ˈdezɪɡneɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>任命；命名</td><td>designate an heir<br>指定继承人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The council designated the whole valley a protected natural area.  议会将整个山谷指定为自然保护区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inaugurate</td><td>/ɪˈnɔːɡjəreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>开始</td><td>inaugurate a new era<br>开创一个新时代</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treaty inaugurated a long period of peace between the two nations.  这项条约开启了两国之间长期的和平。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 induct</td><td>/ɪnˈdʌkt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使正式就职；引入</td><td>induct new members<br>让新成员正式入会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The society inducted five scholars at the annual ceremony.  学会在年度典礼上让五位学者正式入会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 install</td><td>/ɪnˈstɔːl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使就职；安装</td><td>install a new president<br>为新校长举行就职</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The university installed its new president in a formal ceremony.  大学在正式典礼上为新校长举行就职。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nominate</td><td>/ˈnɑːmɪneɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提名，推荐；任命</td><td>nominate a candidate<br>提名一位候选人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The staff nominated her for the annual teaching award.  同事们提名她角逐年度教学奖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dismiss</td><td>/dɪˈsmɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解散，打发走；解雇；不予考虑</td><td>dismiss the class<br>宣布下课</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher dismissed the class ten minutes ahead of time.  老师提前十分钟宣布下课。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 oust</td><td>/aʊst/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>驱逐</td><td>oust the corrupt mayor<br>赶走腐败的市长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board voted to oust the chairman after the scandal became public.  丑闻曝光后，董事会投票罢免了主席。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>instigate</b></td><td>/ˈɪnstɪɡeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>煽动，激起</td><td>instigate a rebellion<br>煽动叛乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several officers were accused of helping to instigate the rebellion in the province.  几名军官被指控参与煽动该省的叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abet</td><td>/əˈbet/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>帮助，教唆（做坏事）</td><td>aid and abet<br>帮助并教唆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was charged with aiding and abetting the robbery.  他被控协助并教唆抢劫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arouse</td><td>/əˈraʊz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>唤起，激起；唤醒</td><td>arouse public suspicion<br>引起公众怀疑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The missing files aroused suspicion among the auditors.  丢失的文件引起了审计人员的怀疑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 excite</td><td>/ɪkˈsaɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激起，引发；使兴奋</td><td>excite public interest<br>激起公众兴趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His speech excited strong resentment among the workers.  他的演讲激起了工人们的强烈不满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 foment</td><td>/foʊˈment/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>助长，煽动</td><td>foment unrest<br>煽动动乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pamphlets were designed to foment rebellion in the provinces.  这些小册子意在煽动各省叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 goad</td><td>/ɡoʊd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>刺激，驱使，激发</td><td>goad him into action<br>激他采取行动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His teammates goaded him into accepting the ridiculous bet.  队友们激他接受了那个荒唐的赌约。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incite</td><td>/ɪnˈsaɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>煽动，激起</td><td>incite a riot<br>煽动暴乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was charged with inciting a riot outside the county courthouse.  他被指控在县法院外煽动暴乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inflame</td><td>/ɪnˈfleɪm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使加剧</td><td>inflame public anger<br>使公众愤怒加剧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor's careless comments only inflamed public anger over the housing shortage.  市长轻率的言论只是加剧了公众对住房短缺的愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 provoke</td><td>/prəˈvoʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激怒；驱使，激起</td><td>provoke an angry response<br>引发愤怒的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His careless remark provoked an angry response from the whole audience.  他不慎的言论激起了全体观众的愤怒回应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stir</td><td>/ˈstɜːr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激起，煽动；搅拌</td><td>stir up anger<br>激起怒火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The article stirred up anger among local residents.  这篇文章激起了当地居民的愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 assuage</td><td>/əˈsweɪdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>缓和，减轻；平息</td><td>assuage his fears<br>减轻他的恐惧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor's speech did little to assuage public anger.  市长的演讲几乎没能平息公众的怒火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 allay</td><td>/əˈleɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，平息</td><td>allay public fears<br>平息公众恐慌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The statement was meant to allay concerns about safety.  这份声明意在减轻人们对安全的担忧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mitigate</td><td>/ˈmɪtɪɡeɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，使缓和</td><td>mitigate the damage<br>减轻损害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Planting trees can mitigate the effects of summer heat.  种树可以减轻夏季高温的影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mollify</td><td>/ˈmɑːlɪfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>平息，抚慰，缓和</td><td>mollify an angry customer<br>安抚愤怒的顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sincere apology from the manager mollified the furious client.  经理诚挚的道歉安抚了这位愤怒的客户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pacify</td><td>/ˈpæsɪfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使平静，安慰</td><td>pacify a crying baby<br>安抚哭闹的婴儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nurse sang softly to pacify the frightened child before the injection.  打针前，护士轻声唱歌安抚受惊的孩子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 soothe</td><td>/suːð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安慰，抚慰；减轻</td><td>soothe a crying baby<br>安抚哭闹的婴儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soft music helped soothe the restless patient through the long night.  轻柔的音乐帮助安抚了焦躁的病人度过漫长的夜晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>instill</b></td><td>/ɪnˈstɪl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>灌输</td><td>instill discipline in children<br>向孩子灌输纪律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Good coaches instill discipline in children without crushing their natural curiosity.  好教练向孩子灌输纪律，却不压抑他们天生的好奇心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 breed</td><td>/ˈbriːd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>引起，酿成；繁殖，养育</td><td>breed resentment<br>滋生怨恨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Secrecy in government breeds distrust among ordinary citizens.  政府的隐秘作风在普通民众中滋生不信任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 enroot</del></td><td>/ɪnˈruːt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使生根，使根深蒂固</td><td>enroot a habit<br>使习惯根深蒂固</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of practice enrooted the melody in his fingers.  多年练习使这段旋律深植于他的指间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 implant</td><td>/ˌɪmˈplænt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>植入，灌输</td><td>implant an idea<br>植入一个想法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film implanted a fear of deep water in many viewers.  这部电影在许多观众心中植入了对深水的恐惧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inculcate</td><td>/ˈɪŋkəlˌkeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>反复灌输，教导</td><td>inculcate good manners<br>反复教导礼貌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her parents inculcated a strong sense of duty in her.  父母在她心中反复灌输强烈的责任感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 infix</td><td>/ɪnˈfɪks/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使铭记，插入</td><td>infix a habit deeply<br>深深植入一种习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of drill infixed the routine so deeply that he could perform it half asleep.  多年操练把这套流程深植于他心中，他半睡半醒都能做出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 infuse</td><td>/ɪnˈfjuːz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>灌输，使…充满</td><td>infuse new energy into<br>注入新的活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new members infused fresh energy into the struggling drama club.  新成员为陷入困境的戏剧社注入了新的活力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ingrain</td><td>/ɪnˈɡreɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使根深蒂固，深植</td><td>ingrain a sense of duty<br>把责任感深植于心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of training ingrained in her the habit of checking every figure twice.  多年训练使她养成了把每个数字复查两遍的习惯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 plant</td><td>/ˈplænt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>种植，栽种；植物；工厂</td><td>plant a row of trees<br>栽种一排树</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Volunteers came to plant young oaks along the muddy riverbank.  志愿者来到泥泞的河岸栽种小橡树。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sow</td><td>/soʊ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>播种；散布</td><td>sow seeds of doubt<br>播下怀疑的种子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rumor quietly sowed distrust among colleagues who had worked together for years.  这则谣言在共事多年的同事间悄悄播下了不信任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 remove</td><td>/rɪˈmuːv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>移除，去掉</td><td>remove all obstacles<br>清除所有障碍</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Workers removed the fallen tree from the road quickly.  工人迅速将倒下的树从路上清除。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>institute</b></td><td>/ˈɪnstɪtuːt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>创立，制定；机构，学院</td><td>institute legal proceedings<br>提起法律诉讼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school instituted a new dress code this term.  学校本学期制定了新的着装规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 begin</td><td>/bɪˈɡɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>开始，着手</td><td>begin work on the project<br>开始着手这个项目</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We will begin the renovation as soon as the permit arrives.  许可一到我们就开始装修。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 constitute</td><td>/ˈkɑːnstətuːt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>指派，设立；构成</td><td>constitute a committee<br>设立委员会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board was properly constituted under the new rules.  依据新规则，董事会得以正式组成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 create</td><td>/kriˈeɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>创造，创立</td><td>create a new department<br>创建新部门</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city created a fund to support local artists.  该市设立了一项基金支持本地艺术家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 found</td><td>/ˈfaʊnd/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>创办，建立</td><td>found a charity<br>创办一家慈善机构</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two students founded the company in a rented garage.  两名学生在租来的车库里创办了这家公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inaugurate</td><td>/ɪˈnɔːɡjəreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>开始</td><td>inaugurate a new era<br>开创一个新时代</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treaty inaugurated a long period of peace between the two nations.  这项条约开启了两国之间长期的和平。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 launch</td><td>/ˈlɔːntʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>发起，开始；发射</td><td>launch a new campaign<br>发起新的活动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company plans to launch its cheapest model this autumn.  公司计划今秋推出其最便宜的型号。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 start</td><td>/ˈstɑːrt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>开始，开端</td><td>from the start<br>从一开始</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 From the start it was clear the schedule was too tight.  从一开始就明显看出日程太紧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 set up</td><td>/sɛt ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>建立，创办；安装，搭建</td><td>set up a company<br>创办一家公司</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They set up a small company in the garage two years ago.  两年前他们在车库里创办了一家小公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abrogate</td><td>/ˈæbrəɡeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>正式废除（法律、条约）</td><td>abrogate a treaty<br>废除条约</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Parliament voted to abrogate the outdated law.  议会投票废除这项过时的法律。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 efface</td><td>/ɪˈfeɪs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>擦掉，抹去；使不显眼</td><td>efface all traces<br>抹去所有痕迹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Time had effaced the inscription on the old gravestone.  时间已把这块老墓碑上的铭文磨去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rescind</td><td>/rɪˈsɪnd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>废除，取消</td><td>rescind an offer<br>撤回报价</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company rescinded the job offer without explanation.  公司未加解释便撤回了聘用通知。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>insubordinate</b></td><td>/ɪnsəˈbɔːrdənət/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不服从的，抗命的</td><td>insubordinate behavior<br>抗命的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The officer was discharged for insubordinate conduct at sea.  这名军官因在海上抗命行为被开除。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 balky</td><td>/ˈbɔːlki/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>执拗的，不肯前进的</td><td>a balky engine<br>不听使唤的引擎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The balky engine refused to start on the coldest morning.  在最冷的那个早晨，这台不听使唤的引擎怎么也发动不起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 contumacious</del></td><td>/ˌkɑːntuˈmeɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不服从的，倔强的</td><td>a contumacious witness<br>不服从的证人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contumacious witness was fined for defying the judge.  那名抗命的证人因违抗法官而被罚款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intractable</td><td>/ˌɪnˈtræktəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难对付的，棘手的</td><td>an intractable problem<br>棘手的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poverty remains an intractable problem in many rural regions.  贫困在许多农村地区仍是棘手的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mutinous</td><td>/ˈmjuːtənəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>反叛的，抗命的</td><td>a mutinous crew<br>抗命的船员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The captain faced a mutinous crew after months at sea.  海上数月后，船长面对一群抗命的船员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recalcitrant</td><td>/rɪˈkælsɪtrənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顽抗的，不顺从的</td><td>a recalcitrant employee<br>不听劝的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A recalcitrant employee refused every instruction his new supervisor gave him.  一名顽抗的员工拒绝了新主管给他的每一条指示。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rebellious</td><td>/rɪˈbeljəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>反叛的，难管束的</td><td>a rebellious teenager<br>叛逆的少年</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her rebellious streak showed early, long before high school.  她的叛逆性格早早就显露，远在高中之前。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 refractory</td><td>/rɪˈfræktəri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>倔强的，不顺从的</td><td>a refractory pupil<br>不顺从的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One refractory pupil disrupted the lesson for the entire class.  一个不顺从的学生扰乱了全班的课。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 amenable</td><td>/əˈmiːnəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，服从的</td><td>amenable to suggestion<br>愿意接受建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new manager is amenable to any reasonable suggestion.  这位新经理对任何合理的建议都乐于接受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 docile</td><td>/ˈdɑːsəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>温顺的，听话的</td><td>a docile animal<br>温顺的动物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old horse was docile enough for beginners to ride.  这匹老马很温顺，初学者也能骑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obedient</td><td>/əˈbiːdiənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>服从的，顺从的</td><td>an obedient child<br>听话的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dog sat quietly, obedient to every hand signal.  那只狗安静地坐着，服从每个手势。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 ruly</del></td><td>/ˈruli/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>守规矩的，服管的</td><td>a ruly classroom<br>守规矩的教室</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new teacher was surprised to find such a ruly classroom.  新老师惊讶地发现这个教室如此守规矩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 submissive</td><td>/səbˈmɪsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>服从的，顺从的</td><td>a submissive attitude<br>顺从的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke in a submissive tone that surprised her colleagues.  她以顺从的语气说话，令同事惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tractable</td><td>/ˈtræktəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易驾驭的，温顺的</td><td>a tractable student<br>听话的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The puppy proved surprisingly tractable during the first weeks of training.  这只小狗在最初几周的训练中出乎意料地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>insular</b></td><td>/ˈɪnsələr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（观念、想法等）孤立狭隘的</td><td>an insular attitude<br>狭隘的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His insular attitude toward foreign customs cost him many friendships abroad.  他对外国习俗的狭隘态度使他在国外失去了许多友谊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 confined</td><td>/kənˈfaɪnd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>狭小的，受限制的</td><td>a confined space<br>狭小的空间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Working in such a confined space made the repair extremely difficult.  在如此狭小的空间作业使维修极为困难。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 local</td><td>/ˈloʊkəl/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>当地的，局部的</td><td>local customs<br>当地风俗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The festival celebrates local food and traditional music.  这个节庆展示当地食物与传统音乐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 narrow</td><td>/ˈnæroʊ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>狭窄的；狭隘的</td><td>a narrow outlook<br>狭隘的眼界</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His narrow reading habits left him ignorant of modern poetry.  他狭窄的阅读习惯使他对现代诗一无所知。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 parochial</td><td>/pəˈroʊkiəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>狭隘的</td><td>a parochial outlook<br>狭隘的眼界</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His parochial outlook kept him from appreciating other cultures.  他狭隘的眼界使他无法欣赏其他文化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 provincial</td><td>/prəˈvɪnʃl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>狭隘（的）</td><td>provincial tastes<br>狭隘的品味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His provincial tastes made him suspicious of anything foreign or unfamiliar.  他狭隘的品味使他对一切外来或陌生的事物都怀疑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 regional</td><td>/ˈriːdʒənəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>地区的，区域的</td><td>regional differences<br>地区差异</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Regional dialects vary greatly across this mountainous province.  在这个多山的省份，各地方言差异很大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 restricted</td><td>/rɪˈstrɪktɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>受限制的；有限的</td><td>a restricted area<br>禁区，限制区域</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only staff with special passes may enter the restricted area.  只有持特别通行证的员工才可进入这一限制区域。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cosmopolitan</td><td>/ˌkɑːzməˈpɑːlɪtən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>有世界性眼光的，包容的</td><td>a cosmopolitan city<br>国际化的都市</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years abroad gave her a cosmopolitan outlook her old classmates clearly lacked.  多年国外生活给了她老同学明显缺乏的世界性眼光。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ecumenical</td><td>/ˌiːkjuːˈmenɪkl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>世界范围的，普遍性的</td><td>an ecumenical council<br>世界性的宗教会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An ecumenical council brought together leaders from every branch of the church.  一次世界性的宗教会议把教会各支派的领袖聚在一起。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>insulate</b></td><td>/ˈɪnsəleɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使绝缘，使隔离，不受外界影响</td><td>insulate children from reality<br>使孩子隔绝现实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wealthy parents often try to insulate their children from everyday hardship.  富裕的父母常常试图让孩子隔绝日常的艰辛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 block</td><td>/ˈblɑːk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>阻碍物；街区</td><td>a block to progress<br>进步的障碍</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fear of failure can be a block to learning anything.  怕失败会成为学习任何东西的障碍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 isolate</td><td>/ˈaɪsəˌleɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>隔离，使孤立</td><td>isolate a patient<br>隔离病人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors isolated the sick passengers as soon as the ship docked.  船一靠岸，医生就隔离了生病的乘客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quarantine</td><td>/ˈkwɔːrəntiːn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>隔离</td><td>place under quarantine<br>予以隔离</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Officials placed the entire flock under quarantine after the outbreak.  疫情爆发后，官员将整群家禽予以隔离。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 seclude</td><td>/səˈkluːd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使隔离，使隐居</td><td>seclude oneself from society<br>使自己与社会隔绝</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The writer secluded himself in a cabin for the winter.  作家整个冬天把自己隔离在小屋里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 segregate</td><td>/ˈseɡrəˌɡeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>隔离，分开</td><td>segregate recyclable waste<br>分离可回收垃圾</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The law once segregated students by race in public schools.  法律曾在公立学校按种族隔离学生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 separate</td><td>/ˈsepəreɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>分开，分离</td><td>separate the yolks<br>把蛋黄分离出来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please separate the recyclable waste from the ordinary rubbish.  请把可回收垃圾与普通垃圾分开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sequester</td><td>/sɪˈkwestər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使隔绝，分离</td><td>sequester the jury<br>隔离陪审团</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge decided to sequester the jury until a verdict was reached.  法官决定隔离陪审团直到作出裁决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 connect</td><td>/kəˈnekt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>连接，联结</td><td>connect the two wires<br>连接两根导线</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A narrow tunnel connects the two halves of the building.  一条窄隧道把大楼的两半连起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 link</td><td>/ˈlɪŋk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>联系，纽带；连接</td><td>a close link<br>紧密的联系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers found a close link between sleep and memory.  研究者发现睡眠与记忆之间存在紧密联系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unite</td><td>/juˈnaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>联合，团结</td><td>unite against a threat<br>联合抵御威胁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crisis united the villagers as nothing else had.  这场危机以前所未有的方式团结了村民。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>insurgent</b></td><td>/ɪnˈsɜːrdʒənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>起义者；叛乱分子</td><td>an armed insurgent<br>武装叛乱分子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Government troops captured three insurgents near the border.  政府军在边境附近抓获三名叛乱分子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rebel</td><td>/rɪˈbel/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>反抗，造反；反叛者</td><td>rebel against authority<br>反抗权威</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students rebelled against the strict new curfew last spring.  学生们去年春天反抗严苛的新宵禁令。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 anarchist</td><td>/ˈænərkɪst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>无政府主义者；反抗权威的人</td><td>an anarchist group<br>一个无政府主义团体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young anarchist refused to recognize any form of state authority.  这位年轻的无政府主义者拒绝承认任何形式的国家权力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 antagonist</td><td>/ænˈtæɡənɪst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>对手，敌人</td><td>a bitter antagonist<br>势不两立的对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her chief antagonist in the debate was a former professor.  她在辩论中的主要对手是一位前教授。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 malcontent</td><td>/ˌmælkənˈtent/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>不满分子；不满的</td><td>a group of malcontents<br>一群不满分子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The general dismissed the critics as a few malcontents.  将军把批评者说成是几个不满分子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mutineer</td><td>/ˌmjuːtəˈnɪr/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>反叛者，叛变士兵</td><td>hang the mutineers<br>处死叛变者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mutineers seized the ship and sailed toward distant islands.  叛变者夺取了船只，向远方的岛屿驶去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>intangible</b></td><td>/ɪnˈtændʒəbl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无法感知的，无形的</td><td>intangible assets<br>无形资产</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A company's reputation is an intangible asset that no balance sheet captures.  公司声誉是一项资产负债表无法体现的无形资产。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impalpable</td><td>/ɪmˈpælpəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以察觉的，无形的</td><td>an impalpable difference<br>难以察觉的差别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A faint, impalpable tension hung over the negotiating table.  谈判桌上笼罩着一种难以察觉的紧张气氛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imperceptible</td><td>/ˌɪmpərˈseptɪbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>察觉不到的，极微小的</td><td>an imperceptible shift<br>察觉不到的变化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The change in temperature was almost imperceptible to the guests.  客人几乎察觉不到温度的变化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imponderable</td><td>/ɪmˈpɑːndərəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>无法估量的，难以捉摸的</td><td>imponderable risks<br>难以估量的风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Forecasters admitted several imponderable factors could change everything.  预测者承认几个难以估量的因素可能改变一切。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 inappreciable</del></td><td>/ˌɪnəˈpriʃəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>微不足道的，感觉不到的</td><td>an inappreciable amount<br>微不足道的量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The risk from such a tiny dose is inappreciable.  如此微小剂量带来的风险微不足道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indiscernible</td><td>/ˌɪndɪˈsɜrnəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以辨别的，看不出的</td><td>an indiscernible difference<br>难以辨别的差异</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The change in color was almost indiscernible to the naked eye.  颜色的变化用肉眼几乎辨别不出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insensible</td><td>/ɪnˈsensəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无知觉的，昏迷的</td><td>insensible to pain<br>感觉不到疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The blow left him insensible for several minutes.  这一击使他几分钟内失去了知觉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 invisible</td><td>/ɪnˈvɪzəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>看不见的，无形的</td><td>invisible to the naked eye<br>肉眼看不见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Radio waves are invisible yet carry sound across continents.  无线电波看不见，却把声音传过大洲。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 corporeal</td><td>/kɔːrˈpɔːriəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>肉体的；有形的，实体的</td><td>corporeal existence<br>肉体的存在</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ghost seemed almost corporeal in the flickering candlelight.  在摇曳的烛光中，那鬼影几乎像有实体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 10

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>integral</b></td><td>/ˈɪntɪɡrəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可缺少的；完整的</td><td>an integral part<br>不可缺少的部分</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Careful revision is an integral part of writing any serious research paper.  仔细修改是撰写任何严肃研究论文不可缺少的部分。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 comprehensive</td><td>/ˌkɑːmprɪˈhensɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>全面的，综合的</td><td>a comprehensive survey<br>全面的调查</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee produced a comprehensive report on water quality.  委员会就水质提出了一份全面的报告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 entire</td><td>/ɪnˈtaɪər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>全部的，完整的</td><td>the entire collection<br>整批收藏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She read the entire manuscript in a single night.  她一夜之间读完了整部手稿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 full</td><td>/ˈfʊl/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>完整的，充满的</td><td>a full account<br>完整的叙述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave a full explanation of the accident to police.  他向警方对事故作了完整说明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grand</td><td>/ˈɡrænd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>宏大的，壮丽的</td><td>a grand staircase<br>宏伟的楼梯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They held the ceremony in a grand marble hall.  他们在宏伟的大理石厅里举行仪式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intact</td><td>/ɪnˈtækt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>完好无损的</td><td>remain intact<br>保持完好</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Remarkably, the vase survived the earthquake completely intact.  令人惊讶的是，这只花瓶在地震中完好无损。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perfect</td><td>/ˈpɜːrfɪkt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>完美的；使完善</td><td>a perfect score<br>满分成绩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He spent years trying to perfect his technique on the cello.  他花了数年时间钻研以完善自己的大提琴技巧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 plenary</td><td>/ˈplenəri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>完全的；全体出席的</td><td>a plenary session<br>全体会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Delegates gathered for the plenary session on Friday morning.  代表们周五上午参加全体会议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 total</td><td>/ˈtoʊtəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>全部的，总计的</td><td>a total failure<br>彻底的失败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The renovation cost a total of two million dollars.  翻修总共花费两百万美元。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 whole</td><td>/ˈhoʊl/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>整个的，完整的</td><td>the whole truth<br>全部真相</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He spent the whole afternoon organizing his notes.  他花了整个下午整理笔记。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 imperfect</td><td>/ɪmˈpɜːrfɪkt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不完美的，有缺陷的</td><td>an imperfect copy<br>不完美的副本</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even an imperfect solution is better than doing nothing at all.  即便是不完美的方案也胜过什么都不做。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incomplete</td><td>/ˌɪnkəmˈpliːt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不完全的，未完成的</td><td>an incomplete list<br>不完整的清单</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The survey remains incomplete because two districts never returned their forms.  调查仍不完整，因为两个区从未交回表格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>integrity</b></td><td>/ɪnˈteɡrəti/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>正直；完整性</td><td>a man of integrity<br>正直的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Everyone respected the judge as a man of unshakable integrity and courage.  所有人都尊敬这位法官，视他为正直不屈且勇气可嘉的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conscience</td><td>/ˈkɑːnʃəns/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>良心，良知</td><td>a guilty conscience<br>内疚的良心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His conscience troubled him long after the lie.  说谎之后很久，他的良心仍在折磨他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 honesty</td><td>/ˈɑːnəsti/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>诚实，正直</td><td>honesty in business<br>生意上的诚信</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her honesty about the mistake earned everyone's respect.  她对错误的坦诚赢得了所有人的尊重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 incorruptibility</del></td><td>/ˌɪnkəˌrʌptəˈbɪləti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>廉洁，不可腐蚀</td><td>judicial incorruptibility<br>司法的廉洁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old judge was famous throughout the province for his incorruptibility.  这位老法官以廉洁在全省闻名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rectitude</td><td>/ˈrektɪtuːd/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>正直；品行端正</td><td>moral rectitude<br>道德上的正直</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old judge was admired for his moral rectitude and quiet courage.  这位老法官因道德上的正直和沉静的勇气而受人敬仰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 righteousness</td><td>/ˈraɪtʃəsnəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>正直，正义</td><td>a sense of righteousness<br>正义感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He defended the poor with a quiet righteousness that impressed everyone.  他以一种沉静的正义感为穷人辩护，令所有人钦佩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 scrupulousness</del></td><td>/ˈskruːpjələsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>严谨，一丝不苟</td><td>scrupulousness in accounting<br>记账的严谨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her scrupulousness about sources impressed the editors greatly.  她对资料来源的严谨令编辑们印象深刻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 completeness</td><td>/kəmˈpliːtnəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>完整，完全</td><td>check for completeness<br>检查是否完整</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The archivist verified the completeness of the donated collection.  档案员核实了这批捐赠藏品的完整性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 entireness</del></td><td>/ɪnˈtaɪərnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>完整，整体性</td><td>the entireness of the work<br>作品的完整性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Restorers preserved the entireness of the ancient mosaic floor.  修复者保住了这片古代马赛克地面的完整。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perfection</td><td>/pərˈfekʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>完美，完善</td><td>strive for perfection<br>追求完美</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The chef rehearsed the dish to perfection before opening night.  厨师在开业前把这道菜练到完美。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wholeness</td><td>/ˈhoʊlnɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>完整，整体</td><td>a sense of wholeness<br>完整感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Therapy helped her recover a feeling of wholeness.  治疗帮她重获一种完整感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 baseness</td><td>/ˈbeɪsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>卑劣，下贱</td><td>the baseness of betrayal<br>背叛的卑劣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The play exposes the baseness hidden beneath polite manners.  这部剧揭露礼貌举止下隐藏的卑劣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>intelligible</b></td><td>/ɪnˈtelɪdʒəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可理解的</td><td>barely intelligible speech<br>几乎听不懂的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Speak slowly so your questions are intelligible to the audience.  慢点说，好让你的问题让听众听得懂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accessible</td><td>/əkˈsesəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>可以到达的；可理解的</td><td>an accessible explanation<br>易懂的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He makes physics accessible to ordinary readers.  他让普通读者也能理解物理学。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 apprehensible</del></td><td>/ˌæprɪˈhensəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可理解的</td><td>readily apprehensible ideas<br>易于理解的观点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His argument is barely apprehensible to readers without training.  他的论证对没有受过训练的读者几乎难以理解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 comprehensible</td><td>/ˌkɑːmpriˈhensəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可理解的，易懂的</td><td>clear and comprehensible<br>清晰易懂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Good teachers make difficult ideas comprehensible to complete beginners.  好老师能让初学者也理解艰深的概念。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 fathomable</del></td><td>/ˈfæðəməbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可理解的，可探明的</td><td>hardly fathomable motives<br>难以捉摸的动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her reasons for leaving were not easily fathomable to us.  她离开的原因我们不容易理解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lucid</td><td>/ˈluːsɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>表达清晰的，易懂的</td><td>a lucid explanation<br>清晰的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor offered a lucid summary of a difficult argument.  教授对一个艰深论证做了清晰的总结。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 understandable</td><td>/ˌʌndərˈstændəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>可理解的；情有可原的</td><td>perfectly understandable<br>完全可以理解</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His anger was understandable given how badly he was treated.  考虑到他受到的糟糕对待，他的愤怒可以理解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abstruse</td><td>/əbˈstruːs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以理解的</td><td>abstruse theories<br>深奥的理论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His lecture was too abstruse for first-year students.  他的讲座对一年级学生来说太晦涩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 recondite</td><td>/ˈrekəndaɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>深奥的，难解的</td><td>recondite arguments<br>深奥的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lecture grew so recondite that half the audience quietly left.  讲座变得如此深奥，一半听众悄悄离场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inscrutable</td><td>/ɪnˈskruːtəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>高深莫测的，难以理解的</td><td>an inscrutable expression<br>高深莫测的表情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His inscrutable smile gave nothing away about his plans.  他那高深莫测的笑容丝毫没有透露他的计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 insensible</td><td>/ɪnˈsensəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无知觉的，昏迷的</td><td>insensible to pain<br>感觉不到疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The blow left him insensible for several minutes.  这一击使他几分钟内失去了知觉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>intemperate</b></td><td>/ɪnˈtempərət/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无节制的，极端的，不温和的</td><td>intemperate language<br>过激的言辞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His intemperate language during the debate cost him the support of moderates.  他在辩论中过激的言辞使他失去了温和派的支持。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 excessive</td><td>/ɪkˈsesɪv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>过度的，过分的</td><td>excessive drinking<br>过量饮酒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors warned him about his excessive salt intake.  医生警告他盐摄入过量。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 extreme</td><td>/ɪkˈstriːm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>极端的，极度的；极端</td><td>extreme views<br>极端的观点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His extreme views cost him most of his old friends.  他极端的观点让他失去了大部分老朋友。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 immoderate</td><td>/ɪˈmɑdərət/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无节制的，过度的</td><td>immoderate drinking<br>无节制的饮酒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her immoderate praise made the young writer distinctly uneasy.  她过度的赞美让这位年轻作家明显不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inordinate</td><td>/ɪnˈɔːrdənət/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过度的，过分的</td><td>an inordinate amount of time<br>过多的时间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He spends an inordinate amount of time arranging his desk.  他花过多时间整理书桌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 overindulgent</del></td><td>/ˌoʊvərɪnˈdʌldʒənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过分放纵的，过分溺爱的</td><td>overindulgent parents<br>过分溺爱的父母</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Overindulgent grandparents spoiled the boy with endless gifts.  过分溺爱的祖父母用无尽的礼物宠坏了这男孩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unrestrained</td><td>/ˌʌnrɪˈstreɪnd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不受约束的；无节制的</td><td>unrestrained spending<br>无节制的开支</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of unrestrained spending finally ruined the company.  多年无节制的开支最终毁掉了这家公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 equable</td><td>/ˈekwəbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（脾气、性情）温和的</td><td>an equable temper<br>平和的性情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His equable nature made him easy to work with.  他性情平和，与他共事很轻松。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>intensify</b></td><td>/ɪnˈtensɪfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>加强，激化</td><td>intensify the pressure<br>加大压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rain intensified just as we reached the summit.  我们刚到山顶，雨就更大了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accentuate</td><td>/əkˈsentʃueɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>强调，突出</td><td>accentuate the difference<br>突出差异</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bright lighting accentuated every flaw in the painting.  明亮的灯光突显出这幅画的每处瑕疵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aggravate</td><td>/ˈæɡrəveɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加重，恶化；激怒</td><td>aggravate the situation<br>使局势恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Running on the injury will only aggravate it.  带着伤跑步只会让伤势加重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 amplify</td><td>/ˈæmpləˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扩大，增强；详述</td><td>amplify a signal<br>放大信号</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Could you amplify your last remark a little?  你能把最后那句话再详述一下吗？</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deepen</td><td>/ˈdiːpən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>加深，深化</td><td>deepen the crisis<br>加深危机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scandal deepened public distrust of the government.  这起丑闻加深了公众对政府的不信任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enhance</td><td>/ɪnˈhæns/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提高，增强，提升</td><td>enhance the flavor<br>提升风味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A little salt greatly enhances the sweetness of the fruit.  少许盐能大大提升水果的甜味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 magnify</td><td>/ˈmæɡnəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>放大；夸大</td><td>magnify the problem<br>夸大问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lens magnifies the specimen four hundred times.  这个镜头把样本放大四百倍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 redouble</td><td>/riːˈdʌbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加倍，大大加强</td><td>redouble one's efforts<br>加倍努力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team redoubled its efforts after the early setback.  早期挫折后，团队加倍努力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 strengthen</td><td>/ˈstreŋθən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>加强，增强</td><td>strengthen the economy<br>增强经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Regular exercise will strengthen your heart and lungs.  规律锻炼会增强你的心肺功能。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abate</td><td>/əˈbeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>减轻，减弱；减少</td><td>the storm gradually abated<br>风暴逐渐减弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fever finally abated after two days of medication.  服药两天后，烧终于退了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 assuage</td><td>/əˈsweɪdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>缓和，减轻；平息</td><td>assuage his fears<br>减轻他的恐惧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor's speech did little to assuage public anger.  市长的演讲几乎没能平息公众的怒火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 attenuate</td><td>/əˈtenjueɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>减弱，衰减；变细</td><td>attenuate the signal strength<br>减弱信号强度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick concrete walls attenuate the radio signal inside the building.  厚厚的混凝土墙削弱了建筑内部的无线电信号。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mitigate</td><td>/ˈmɪtɪɡeɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，使缓和</td><td>mitigate the damage<br>减轻损害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Planting trees can mitigate the effects of summer heat.  种树可以减轻夏季高温的影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 moderate</td><td>/ˈmɑːdəreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>缓和，减轻；适度的</td><td>moderate one's demands<br>降低自己的要求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He agreed to moderate his tone during the debate.  他同意在辩论中缓和语气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>inter</b></td><td>/ɪnˈtɜːr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>埋葬</td><td>inter the body<br>埋葬尸体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The villagers gathered at dawn to inter the body beside the old chapel.  村民们黎明时聚集，把遗体埋葬在古老的小教堂旁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bury</td><td>/ˈberi/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>埋葬，掩埋</td><td>bury the treasure<br>埋藏宝藏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Squirrels bury nuts all over the garden every autumn.  松鼠每年秋天把坚果埋在花园各处。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 entomb</td><td>/ɪnˈtuːm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>埋葬，掩埋</td><td>entomb the dead<br>埋葬死者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ash from the eruption entombed the entire village.  火山喷发的灰烬埋葬了整个村庄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 inhume</del></td><td>/ɪnˈhjum/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>埋葬，土葬</td><td>inhume the dead<br>埋葬死者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The villagers inhumed their dead on the hillside above the river.  村民把亡者葬在河上方的山坡上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dig</td><td>/ˈdɪɡ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>挖，掘</td><td>dig a deep hole<br>挖一个深坑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They dug through frozen soil to reach the broken pipe.  他们挖穿冻土才到达破裂的管道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 disinter</del></td><td>/ˌdɪsɪnˈtɜːr/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（从墓地里）掘出</td><td>disinter the remains<br>掘出遗骸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Archaeologists received permission to disinter the remains beneath the old chapel floor.  考古学家获准掘出旧礼拜堂地板下的遗骸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 excavate</td><td>/ˈekskəveɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>挖掘，挖空</td><td>excavate an ancient tomb<br>挖掘古墓</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Archaeologists excavated the ancient tomb layer by careful layer.  考古学家一层一层小心地挖掘那座古墓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exhume</td><td>/eksˈhjuːm/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>掘出（尸体），发掘</td><td>exhume a body<br>掘出尸体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Investigators exhumed the remains for further forensic testing.  调查人员掘出遗骸以做进一步法医检验。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unearth</td><td>/ʌnˈɜːrθ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>挖出，发掘；发现</td><td>unearth ancient coins<br>挖出古代钱币</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Archaeologists unearthed a marble head near the temple wall.  考古学家在神庙墙边挖出一个大理石头像。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>intercessor</b></td><td>/ˌɪntərˈsesər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>调停者</td><td>serve as an intercessor<br>充当调停者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A respected elder served as intercessor between the two feuding families.  一位受人尊敬的长者充当了两个敌对家族之间的调停者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 broker</td><td>/ˈbroʊkər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>经纪人；中间人</td><td>an insurance broker<br>保险经纪人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A neutral broker helped the two sides reach an agreement.  一位中立的中间人帮助双方达成协议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 buffer</td><td>/ˈbʌfər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>缓冲物；起缓冲作用</td><td>act as a buffer<br>起缓冲作用</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A row of trees serves as a buffer against street noise.  一排树成了隔挡街道噪音的缓冲。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conciliator</td><td>/kənˈsɪlieɪtər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>调解人，调停者</td><td>an impartial conciliator<br>公正的调解人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The union asked a professional conciliator to join the talks.  工会请了一位专业调解人参与谈判。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intermediate</td><td>/ˌɪntərˈmiːdiət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>中间的；中间物</td><td>an intermediate stage<br>中间阶段</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reaction produces an intermediate compound that quickly breaks down again.  这个反应产生一种很快又分解的中间化合物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mediator</td><td>/ˈmiːdieɪtər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>调解人，中间人</td><td>a neutral mediator<br>中立的调解人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A court-appointed mediator settled the long dispute between neighbors.  法院指定的调解人解决了邻里间的长期纠纷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 peacemaker</td><td>/ˈpiːsmeɪkər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>调解人，和事佬</td><td>play the peacemaker<br>充当和事佬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She often played peacemaker between her quarreling brothers.  她常在争吵的兄弟间充当和事佬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 flame-fanner</del></td><td>/ˈfleɪm ˌfænər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>煽风点火者</td><td>a notorious flame-fanner<br>出名的煽风点火者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Every dispute attracted the same flame-fanner eager to worsen things.  每场争执都吸引同一个急于把事情搞糟的煽风点火者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>interdict</b></td><td>/ˌɪntərˈdɪkt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>禁止；拦截；封锁</td><td>interdict arms shipments<br>禁止武器运输</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Naval patrols were sent to interdict arms shipments along the coast.  海军巡逻队被派去拦截沿海的军火运输。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ban</td><td>/ˈbæn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>禁止；禁令</td><td>ban smoking indoors<br>禁止室内吸烟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city voted to ban plastic bags in all supermarkets.  该市投票决定禁止所有超市使用塑料袋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 forbid</td><td>/fərˈbɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>禁止，不许</td><td>forbid smoking indoors<br>禁止室内吸烟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 School rules forbid students to leave campus during the lunch break.  校规禁止学生在午餐时间离开校园。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prohibit</td><td>/proʊˈhɪbət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>禁止，阻止</td><td>prohibit the sale of<br>禁止销售</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city prohibits overnight parking on this narrow street.  该市禁止在这条窄街上过夜停车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 proscribe</td><td>/proʊˈskraɪb/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>禁止，排斥</td><td>proscribe a political party<br>取缔某个政党</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new regime moved quickly to proscribe every opposition political party.  新政权迅速采取行动取缔所有反对党。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 authorize</td><td>/ˈɔːθəˌraɪz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>授权，批准，委托</td><td>authorize the payment<br>批准这笔付款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only the director can authorize spending above that limit.  只有主管能批准超出该限额的开支。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>interim</b></td><td>/ˈɪntərɪm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>中间过渡时期，间隔；暂时的</td><td>an interim report<br>中期报告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee published an interim report while the investigation was still continuing.  委员会在调查仍在进行时发布了一份中期报告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 breach</td><td>/briːtʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>违背</td><td>breach the contract<br>违约</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company breached the contract by delivering the goods late.  这家公司延迟交货，违反了合同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 break</td><td>/ˈbreɪk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>损坏，打破；间歇</td><td>break the vase<br>打破花瓶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cat broke the vase while chasing a moth indoors.  猫在屋里追蛾子时打破了花瓶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gap</td><td>/ˈɡæp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>缺口，隘口；差距</td><td>a gap in the hills<br>山间的隘口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A narrow gap in the ridge let the river through.  山脊上一道狭窄的缺口让河水穿过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 interruption</td><td>/ˌɪntəˈrʌpʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>中断，打扰</td><td>without interruption<br>不受打扰地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She worked for three hours without a single interruption.  她连续工作三小时未被打扰一次。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 interval</td><td>/ˈɪntərvəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>间隔，间歇</td><td>at regular intervals<br>每隔一定时间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Buses arrive at ten-minute intervals during rush hour.  高峰时段公交每隔十分钟一班。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 interlude</td><td>/ˈɪntərluːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>间隙，插曲</td><td>a brief interlude<br>短暂的间隙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The trip was a pleasant interlude in a very busy year.  这次旅行是忙碌一年中愉快的插曲。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 parenthesis</td><td>/pəˈrenθəsɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>间断</td><td>a brief parenthesis<br>短暂的间断</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The war was only a brief parenthesis in the peaceful history of the town.  那场战争只是这个小镇平静历史中一段短暂的间断。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 acting</td><td>/ˈæktɪŋ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>代理的，临时的</td><td>the acting director<br>代理主管</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She served as acting mayor for six months.  她担任代理市长六个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 provisional</td><td>/prəˈvɪʒənl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>临时的</td><td>a provisional agreement<br>临时协议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two sides signed a provisional agreement pending final legal review.  双方签署了一份临时协议，等待最终的法律审查。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 temporary</td><td>/ˈtempəreri/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>暂时的，临时的</td><td>a temporary solution<br>临时的解决办法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company hired temporary staff to handle the busy holiday season.  公司雇了临时员工来应对繁忙的假日季。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 continuation</td><td>/kənˌtɪnjuˈeɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>继续，延续</td><td>the continuation of talks<br>会谈的继续</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The continuation of the project depends on further funding.  项目的继续取决于后续资金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 continuity</td><td>/ˌkɑːntəˈnuːəti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>连续性，持续</td><td>ensure continuity of service<br>保证服务的连续</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The handover plan preserves continuity in patient care.  交接方案保证了病患护理的连续性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 eternal</td><td>/ɪˈtɜːrnl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的</td><td>eternal life<br>永生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poem promises the beloved eternal youth.  这首诗许诺爱人永恒的青春。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 permanent</td><td>/ˈpɜːrmənənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的</td><td>a permanent solution<br>永久的解决办法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They are seeking a permanent solution rather than another quick fix.  他们在寻找永久的解决办法，而不是又一个权宜之计。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>interlock</b></td><td>/ˌɪntərˈlɑːk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>连锁，连结</td><td>interlocking gears<br>相互咬合的齿轮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The gears interlock so precisely that the machine runs almost without noise.  这些齿轮咬合得如此精确，机器运转几乎没有噪音。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 associate</td><td>/əˈsoʊʃieɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>联合，结交；联想</td><td>associate with the group<br>与该团体结交</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He refused to associate himself with such a risky plan.  他拒绝与这样冒险的计划有牵连。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 connect</td><td>/kəˈnekt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>连接，联结</td><td>connect the two wires<br>连接两根导线</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A narrow tunnel connects the two halves of the building.  一条窄隧道把大楼的两半连起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 join</td><td>/ˈdʒɔɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>连接，接合；加入</td><td>join the two pieces<br>把两块接起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Join the ends of the rope with a firm knot.  用牢固的结把绳子两端接起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 link</td><td>/ˈlɪŋk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>联系，纽带；连接</td><td>a close link<br>紧密的联系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers found a close link between sleep and memory.  研究者发现睡眠与记忆之间存在紧密联系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unite</td><td>/juˈnaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>联合，团结</td><td>unite against a threat<br>联合抵御威胁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crisis united the villagers as nothing else had.  这场危机以前所未有的方式团结了村民。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sunder</td><td>/ˈsʌndər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>分开，割断</td><td>sunder the alliance<br>使联盟破裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bitter dispute sundered an alliance that had lasted decades.  这场激烈的争端割断了维持数十年的联盟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>
